  - **Viewport page (`SettingsPageViewport`, `OpenViewportSettings`)**: edits **Snap** (toggle + grid step in `px` + rotation angle in `°`, hold `⌘` to ignore), **Grid** (dots/grid every snap step, `SetShowGrid`), **Show agent changes** (`Editor.ShowAgentChanges`, `Editor.SetShowAgentChanges`: outlines in the viewport while a review is open), **3D navigation** (`Editor.Navigation3D`, `Editor.SetNavigation3D`, `Navigation3D.Resolve`: `Default` — left-drag orbit / Shift+left-drag or middle-drag pan / right-drag fly; `Blender` — middle-drag orbit / Shift+middle-drag pan / right-drag fly / left-drag select & gizmos; `Maya` — Alt+left-drag orbit / middle-drag or Alt+middle-drag pan / Alt+right-drag dolly / plain right-drag fly / plain left-drag select & gizmos; wheel dollies and touch/trackpad gestures stay unchanged in all modes), and every toggleable **Overlay legend** choice (`ViewportOverlays`, persisted per computer).
  - **Keyboard and accessibility**: `ArrowDown` / `ArrowUp` / `Home` / `End` move through the left navigation list; every on/off toggle uses `SpecSwitch` (`RoleSwitch` with `aria-checked` and a 2 px Spec accent focus ring); every row is reachable with `Tab`.
- **Automatic saving and status bar save status (#1016)**:
  - **General › Save automatically (`Editor.AutoSave`, `Editor.SetAutoSave`)**: enabled by default and persisted as `autoSave` in `EditorSettings`. Kept changes to file-backed scenes and `game.yaml` save automatically after a 2-second debounce (`DefaultAutoSaveDelay`, configurable via `Editor.AutoSaveDelay`). Edits inside an open review (`Editor.HasOpenReview()`, including `BeginReview` / `KeepReview`, cutscene reviews, playtest metric reviews, safe rename reviews, merge conflicts, and unresolved disk conflicts) are not kept yet and pause autosave until kept.
  - **Status bar save item (`Editor.SaveStatus`, `SaveStatusSaved` / `SaveStatusSaving` / `SaveStatusFailed`)**: displays muted `Saved` (hover tooltip shows the last save time), transitions to `Saving…` only after a write has stayed in flight for at least 300 ms (`DefaultSavingIndicatorDelay`, configurable via `Editor.SavingIndicatorDelay`) so quick saves never flicker, and keeps failed autosaves in the status bar (never as a toast) with a human-readable cause (such as `"Couldn't save: folder is read-only"` or `"Couldn't save: disk is full · 12 MB free"`), plus **Retry** (`Editor.RetrySave`) and **Save as…** actions until fixed.
  - **While saving fails**: the unsaved dot beside the scene name and on the failing scene tab turns Spec problem red (`#FF6B6B`), quitting asks first (`Editor.HasUnsavedChanges()` remains `true`), and `editormcp` appends a warning to every tool result until a save succeeds.
- **Live language switching**: `Language` accepts `LanguageSystem` (`"system"`), `LanguageEnglish` (`"en"`), or `LanguageChinese` (`"zh"`). Calling `Editor.SetLanguage` or picking a language on the **General** page under **`EDITOR · THIS COMPUTER`** in the unified **Settings** view (`SettingsView` / `EditorSettingsDialogView`, opened via `File > Editor Settings...`, Command Palette `CmdEditorSettings`, or `mod+,`) updates the header bar, desktop menu bar, file browser, asset browser, scene tree, layers, inspector, bottom panels, dialogs, command palette, and status bar immediately without restarting. `Editor.ResolvedLanguage()` reports the active locale (`"en"` or `"zh"`) and is exposed to connected MCP agents through `selection_get`.
- **Appearance (`core.Scope.SetAppearance`) and Spec Dark/Light theme (#1062)**: `Appearance` accepts `AppearanceSystem` (`"system"`), `AppearanceLight` (`"light"`), or `AppearanceDark` (`"dark"`). `EditorView` installs the embedded YAML Spec theme (`theme.yaml`, loaded via `core.ParseTheme` and exposed through `theme.Core`, `theme.Dark`, `theme.Light`, `theme.PaletteFor`, and `Editor.Palette`) onto the root scope so every NSGo `ui` widget, panel, viewport overlay, overlay legend (`SpecOverlayLegendView` / `SetOverlayLegendOpen`), and toast (`ShowToast` / `DismissToast` / `ToastView`) follows the Spec tokens in both Dark and Light appearances instead of falling back to NSGo's default theme. Every foreground/background, button-on-fill, warning-card, and viewport-overlay pair in both palettes is measured against the same WCAG 2.2 contrast table (`theme.ContrastRatio`, `theme.ContrastTable`: >= 4.5:1 AA for text, pill labels, and text on accent/agent/problem fills; >= 3:1 UI contrast for focus rings and viewport overlay edges). With `AppearanceSystem`, changing the OS appearance repaints the editor live without a restart; explicit `AppearanceLight` or `AppearanceDark` choices persist to `Editor.SettingsPath` across restarts.
- **Shortcuts: searchable and rebindable within a scope (#1061)**:
  - **Command scopes**: every `EditorCommand` and `PluginCommand` carries a `Scope` (`ScopeGlobal`, `ScopeViewport`, `ScopeViewport2D`, `ScopeViewport3D`, `ScopeSceneTree`, `ScopeTile`, `ScopeAnimation`, `ScopePlay`, defaulting to `ScopeGlobal` when omitted).
  - **Settings › Shortcuts (`EditorSettingsTabShortcuts`, `OpenShortcutsSettings`, `CmdShortcutsSettings`)**: lists every registered built-in, custom, and active plugin command with its scope and live formatted key, searchable by command name, key/shortcut, or scope (`FilterShortcutCommands`). Pressing a command's key binding enters key-capture mode (`ShortcutFromKeyEvent`, cancelled cleanly with `Escape` without closing the dialog). Rebinding to a key already used in the **same** scope (`ShortcutConflict` / `*ShortcutConflictError`) flags the conflict in place with a **Swap** button (`SwapShortcut`, leaving both commands bound to each other's keys); binding a key used only in a **different** scope (`RebindShortcut`) succeeds immediately without a prompt. Per-row **Reset** (`ResetShortcut`) and **Reset all** (`ResetAllShortcuts`) restore defaults.
  - **Persistence and live surfaces**: custom bindings persist under `shortcuts` in `EditorSettings` (`editor.yaml`, outside any project) and immediately update `EffectiveShortcut`, header bar tooltips/labels, the desktop menu bar (`ApplicationMenu`), Command Palette (`CommandPaletteView`), runtime key bindings, and the **`⌘/` Keyboard Shortcuts sheet** (`ShortcutsSheetView`, `CmdShortcutsSheet` / `mod+/`).
- **Plugin localization**: plugins may attach a `Catalog *l10n.Catalog` or versioned `LocaleYAML [][]byte` string tables (`RegisterPlugin` validates them strictly) and set optional `TitleKey` / `LabelKey` / `DescriptionKey` / `CategoryKey` on their panels, tools, and commands. Inside `Panel.View` and `Inspector.View`, plugins call `Editor.PluginT(pluginName, key, args...)` and `Editor.PluginPlural(...)` so all plugin UI follows the editor's active language live.

## 2D skeletal rig editor (#886)

The editor opens, edits, previews, saves, and places version-1 `.skel.yaml` 2D skeletal rigs (`game/skeletal`) directly through `RigPanelView` below the viewport:

- **Opening and lossless round-tripping**: select any `*.skel.yaml` or `*.skel.yml` file in `FilesView` and double-click it or press **Open Rig** (`OpenRigProjectFile`, `OpenRigFile`, `OpenRigYAML`), or start a fresh rig with `NewRig`. Opening and saving an existing rig without edits (or after undoing every edit back to the opened state) writes back the exact original bytes unchanged (`SaveRigYAML`, `SaveRigFile`, `Editor.OnSaveRig`); edited rigs serialize deterministically via `skeletal.Encode`.
- **Interactive rig canvas and handles**: `DrawRigViewport` renders attachments through the runtime `skeletal.Skeleton.Draw` beneath the bone hierarchy and interactive handles:
  - **Bone Move (`RigHandleBoneMove`)**: drag a bone's joint circle to move it in its parent's local space (in Bind Pose) or key its `translate` offset at the current playhead when an animation is active.
  - **Bone Rotate (`RigHandleBoneRotate`)**: drag the rotation ring around a bone to rotate it (bind pose or active animation `rotate` keyframe).
  - **Bone Length (`RigHandleBoneLength`)**: drag the tip diamond to set `BoneData.Length`.
  - **Attachment Origin (`RigHandleAttachmentOrigin`)**: drag the pivot crosshair on the selected slot's active skin attachment to adjust `OriginX` and `OriginY`.
  - Every handle drag (`BeginRigDrag`, `BeginRigHandleDrag`, `UpdateRigDrag`, `EndRigDrag`) commits as one undoable `Editor.History` step, and `CancelRigDrag` (or pressing `Escape` during a drag) restores the pre-drag snapshot without touching history.
- **Bones, slots, skins, and attachments**: add (`AddRigBone`), rename (`RenameRigBone`), delete (`DeleteRigBone`), move/rotate/scale (`MoveRigBone`, `RotateRigBone`, `ScaleRigBone`, `SetRigBoneLength`), and reparent bones (`ReparentRigBone`, optionally preserving bind-pose world transforms and refusing cycles); add, delete, rebind, fade, and reorder draw slots (`AddRigSlot`, `DeleteRigSlot`, `SetRigSlotBone`, `SetRigSlotAlpha`, `MoveRigSlotOrder`); manage skins (`AddRigSkin`, `DeleteRigSkin`, `SelectRigSkin`) and attach atlas frames with origin pivots (`AttachRigFrame`, `SetRigAttachment`, `SetRigAttachmentOrigin`, `SetRigAttachmentTransform`, `RemoveRigAttachment`).
- **Timeline, playback, and crossfade preview**: create animations (`AddRigAnimation`, `DeleteRigAnimation`, `SelectRigAnimation`, `SetRigAnimationDuration`, `SetRigAnimationLoop`), key bone `translate`, `rotate`, `scale` and slot `attachment` and `alpha` tracks (`KeyRigBoneTranslate`, `KeyRigBoneRotate`, `KeyRigBoneScale`, `KeyRigSlotAttachment`, `KeyRigSlotAlpha`, `DeleteRigBoneKeyframe`, `DeleteRigSlotKeyframe`), scrub (`SeekRig`), play (`PlayRig`, `PauseRig`, `StopRig`, `StepRig`), and preview crossfade blends (`CrossfadeRig`, `RigBlending`) directly through the runtime `skeletal.Skeleton` (`RigSkeleton`).
- **Placing rigs in scenes**: press **Place in Scene** (`PlaceOpenRig`, `PlaceRig`) to add a scene object carrying the built-in `"skeletal"` component (`sceneformat.SkeletalComponent`, `version: 1`, `rig`, `atlas`, `skin`, `animation`, `loop`). `sceneformat.Build` loads the rig through `SkeletonResolver` or `FileResolver` (`FSAssets`), builds a live `BuiltObject.Skeleton` with the chosen skin and animation, draws it in exact Z order in both `BuiltScene.Draw` and the 2D editor viewport, and advances it in `BuiltScene.Update(dt)`. `refs.Scan` tracks `components.skeletal.rig` (`refs.FieldSkeletalRig`) so `MoveFile` rewrites rig paths across scenes automatically.

## HUD and menu layout screen (#1047)

Open the HUD and menu layout screen (`HUDLayoutView`) via `Project > HUD & Menu Layout`, Command Palette (`mod+shift+u` / `CmdHUDLayout`), double-clicking or opening any `*.screen.yaml` file in the file browser, or clicking a screen diagnostic in the Problems panel (`OpenHUDScreen` / `OpenHUDScreenFile` / `OpenHUDScreenDoc`). Styled strictly with the editor's design spec tokens (`design/editor/HUDLayout.dc.html` and `Spec.dc.html`), it previews every screen size and project language side by side while keeping manual layout edits focused and undoable:

- **Header and Language switcher**: displays the `<project> / screens / <file>` breadcrumb (with a back button returning to the scene editor), a **Language** segmented control listing every locale from `locales/*.yaml` (and any unsaved `Editor.l10n` workspace edits) via `SetHUDLocale`, **Search ⌘K**, and **Play**. Switching languages immediately redraws all four screen sizes, the text-width table, the inspector's localized values and overflow zoom, and **GAME CHECK**.
- **Left rail (`SCREENS` and `NODES`)**: lists all project `*.screen.yaml` files (`HUDScreens`) with each screen's node count and active-language problem count, plus the active screen's hierarchical node tree (`HUDTree`) with monospace anchor tags (`TL`, `TC`, `TR`, `CL`, `C`, `CR`, `BL`, `BC`, `BR`, `FILL`) and red problem dots.
- **Four simultaneous device frames and overlays**: draws the active screen at **Phone 19.5:9 with a notch** (`780 × 360`), **Tablet 4:3** (`640 × 480`), **Desktop 16:9** (`640 × 360`), and **Ultrawide 21:9** (`840 × 360`) at once through real `gameui.BuildScreen` layout (`HUDLayoutFrames`), with switches for **Safe areas**, **Anchors**, **Touch areas**, and **Focus order** (`HUDOverlays` / `ToggleHUDOverlay`), plus a floating **TEXT WIDTH, PX** table (`HUDTextWidths`) comparing each text node's box width against its measured width in every locale with overflows highlighted.
- **Inspector, zoomed overflow detail, and `GAME CHECK`**: inspects the selected node's **ANCHOR** (a 3×3 grid of buttons plus **Fill**, anchor and offset in words, and **Keep inside the safe area**), **SIZE** (**Fixed** / **Fit text** toggle and touch target size), **TEXT** (key, every locale's value, and a `1.5×` zoomed overflow detail card when localized text spills past its box), and live **GAME CHECK** diagnostics backed by `gameui.CheckScreen` (`HUDProblems`) with **Show** buttons to select offending nodes, **Ask the agent to fix N**, and an empty `"No problems at any size in this language"` state when clean.
- **Manual edits and agent composer**: reordering children inside a container (`ReorderHUDNode`), adjusting container spacing (`SetHUDNodeSpacing`), changing anchor/safe-area/sizing (`SetHUDNodeAnchor`, `SetHUDNodeSafeArea`, `SetHUDNodeSizeMode`), and directly dragging touch controls (`BeginHUDTouchDrag`, `UpdateHUDTouchDrag`, `EndHUDTouchDrag`, `CancelHUDTouchDrag`) each record **one undo step** in `Editor.History` and write canonical YAML back to the `*.screen.yaml` file on disk (restored verbatim on Undo). A bottom composer (`SubmitHUDPrompt`, `AskAgentToFixHUDProblems`, `Editor.OnHUDPrompt`) forwards broader HUD changes to the agent.

## Cutscene timeline and agent review (#1056)

Opening any `*.cutscene.yaml` / `*.cutscene.yml` file (from the file browser, Command Palette, `OpenCutsceneProjectFile`, `OpenCutsceneFile`, `OpenCutsceneYAML`, or an agent `cutscene_write` MCP call) opens the full-workspace cutscene timeline editor (`CutscenePanelView`), styled strictly with `Spec.dc.html` design tokens and laid out from Screen 2 of `design/editor/Animation.dc.html`:

- **Left rail (`CUTSCENES` and `LINES`)**: lists every project cutscene (`ProjectCutscenes`) with its length or `video` kind marker, plus every localized dialogue line (`CutsceneLines`) resolved from `loc/en.yaml` and `loc/zh.yaml` (or `Editor.CutsceneCatalog` / the open Localization workspace) showing its line ID, English text, and Chinese text.
- **Live preview stage and thumbnail scrub strip**: runs a live `cutscene.TimelinePlayer` against an isolated copy of the scene (`CutscenePreviewScene`, `SeekCutscene`, `PlayCutscene`, `PauseCutscene`, `StepCutscene`) so scrubbing to any timestamp poses actors, camera, and subtitle identically to runtime playback. Displays the active camera caption (`CutsceneCameraLabel`), localized subtitle overlay (`CutsceneActiveDialogue`), Play/Pause transport, time readout, and a 24-frame clickable thumbnail strip (`CutsceneThumbs`).
- **Track lanes and clip inspector**: displays ordered lanes for Camera, each actor, Dialogue, Sound, and Music tracks with positioned clip blocks. Selecting a clip (`SelectCutsceneClip`, `SelectCutsceneClipByID`) inspects and edits **Start** and **Duration** with `±0.1 s` steppers (`AdjustCutsceneClipStart`, `AdjustCutsceneClipDuration`, `SetCutsceneClipTiming`) and **Easing** (`Linear`, `In`, `Out`, `In-out`, `Hold` via `SetCutsceneClipEasing`) on camera and actor tracks. Selecting a dialogue clip displays its line ID, English and Chinese text, and the reminder that text lives in `loc/en.yaml` and `loc/zh.yaml` while the cutscene stores only the ID.
- **Agent review mode (`OpenCutsceneReview`)**: compares the original cutscene (`Before`) against the agent's proposal (`After`) without overwriting the file on disk until accepted. Supports toggling **Before** / **After** (`SetCutsceneReviewViewMode`) or holding **B** to peek at Before (`SetCutsceneReviewPeekBefore`, `CutsceneHandleKeyEvent`), marks added and changed clips with dashed line styles (`added` green dashed, `changed` amber dashed, `selected` blue solid) alongside the legend, lists `N CHANGES` with per-change **Revert** / **Restore** (`ToggleCutsceneReviewChange`) and click-to-focus (`FocusCutsceneReviewChange`), lets manual clip edits join the active review with `⌘Z` undo (`"Your edit joins this review · ⌘Z undoes it"`), offers **Revert all** (`RevertAllCutsceneReview`), and commits the remaining kept changes and joined edits on **Keep N of M** (`KeepCutsceneReview`) in **one atomic undo step** (both in memory and on disk).

## Changes and commit panel (#1057)

Open the full-window **Changes and commit** screen (`ChangesPanelView`) from the status bar's **Changes** entry (showing the live working-tree changed-file count), `Project > Changes & Commit`, or Command Palette (`CmdChanges` / `OpenChangesPanel` / `CloseChangesPanel`), matching `design/editor/VersionControl.dc.html` (screen 1) using only `Spec.dc.html` design tokens:

- **Branch switcher, upstream status, and Pull**: the header displays `<project> / <scene>`, a branch switcher dropdown (`GitSwitchBranch`) showing the active branch plus ahead/behind counts (`ahead N`, `behind M`), **Refresh** (`RefreshChanges`), **Pull** (`GitPull`, fast-forward only), and **Play**.
- **CHANGES grouped by category**: working-tree changes against `HEAD` (`git status --porcelain=v1 -uall`) group under **SCENES**, **DATA**, **CODE**, and **ASSETS** (`ChangesCategory`), each file carrying an include checkbox (`SetChangeIncluded`, `ToggleChangeIncluded`), status badge (`M`, `A`, `D`, `R`), and a concise detail line (`4 added · 3 changed objects`, `+7 -4 lines`, or audio duration and file size).
- **Per-file comparison with `HEAD` in the Review layout**:
  - **Scenes (`*.scene.yaml` / `*.scene.yml`)**: compares `HEAD` (`git show HEAD:<path>`) with the working tree via side-by-side **HEAD** and **Now** 2D scene previews (plus single-view **HEAD** / **Now** toggles via `SetChangesSceneView`), outlining added objects in dashed green, changed objects in dashed amber, and removed objects in dashed red, with per-object change badges (`added · ...`, `changed · Door (x 584 → 560)`, `removed · ...`).
  - **Data and Go code**: displays the latest `nsgo check` and `nsgo test` status and relative timestamp (`RunChangesChecks`, injectable via `Editor.ChangesCheckRunner`) above a color-coded unified line-by-line diff (`git diff HEAD -- <path>`, or full `+` lines for untracked files) and an **Open in editor** button (`OpenChangedFileInEditor`).
  - **Audio assets**: renders a playable audition card (`AuditionChangedAudio`, `StopChangedAudio`) with duration, format/sample-rate metadata, a 60-bar waveform, and incoming references (`Used by ...`) gathered from the project reference index and scene objects.
- **Selective commit card and History milestones**: drafts a commit summary and description from the included files, review/playtest context (`SetChangesContext`), and checks via `Editor.CommitMessageDrafter` or deterministic `DefaultCommitDraft` with **Redraft** (`RedraftCommitMessage`), editable summary and description inputs (`SetCommitSummary`, `SetCommitDescription`), `"Made from N agent requests, reviewed and kept"` attribution when present, included `+/−` line counts, **Commit** (`CommitSelectedChanges`), **Commit and push** (`CommitAndPushSelectedChanges`), and **Push N commits** (`PushCommits`). Committing stages and commits **only** the checked files (`git add -A -- <included>` + `git commit --only -m ... -- <included>`), leaving every unchecked file untouched and uncommitted, and records a `CommitMilestone` in `Editor.History` (`History.Milestones` / `CommitMilestones`). Nothing is ever committed or pushed automatically without user action.
- **SHARE**: **Copy as PR description** (`CopyPRDescription` / `BuildPRDescription`, writing structured Markdown with summary, changed files, checks, and playtest numbers via `core.Scope.SetClipboard` or `Editor.ChangesClipboard`) and **Open pull request** (`OpenPullRequest`, enabled when `gh` is on `PATH` or `Editor.GHRunner` is set).

## Scene merge conflict resolution (#1059, #1531)

When Git leaves an unmerged scene file — during `git merge`, `git rebase`, `git pull --rebase`, `git cherry-pick`, `git revert`, or `git stash pop` (whether written by the object-by-object `go tool game merge` driver from #1058 or by Git's standard text conflict markers) — opening the project (`OpenProject`), pressing the status-bar conflict pill, or invoking `Project > Resolve Scene Merge Conflicts` (`CmdMergeConflicts` / `OpenMergeConflict`) opens the full-window **Scene merge conflict** screen (`MergeConflictView`, styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `VersionControl.dc.html` screen 2):

- **Git operation detection and stage mapping**: detects `MERGE_HEAD`, `REBASE_HEAD` (or `.git/rebase-merge` / `rebase-apply`), `CHERRY_PICK_HEAD`, `REVERT_HEAD`, and unmerged index entries without a `*_HEAD` ref (`git stash pop` / `apply`). During a `rebase` or `pull --rebase`, stages `:2:` and `:3:` are swapped relative to the author so stage `:3:` is shown as **Mine (commit being replayed)** and stage `:2:` is shown as **Upstream**; cherry-pick and revert name the picked or reverted commit.
- **Header and progress**: displays operation-specific progress (`Merging <theirs> into <mine>`, `Rebasing <mine> onto <upstream>`, `Cherry-picking <sha> onto <mine>`, `Reverting <sha> on <mine>`, or `Applying stash@{0} onto <mine>` with `N of M objects need you`), the matching abort button (`AbortMerge` running `git merge --abort`, `git rebase --abort`, `git cherry-pick --abort`, `git revert --abort`, or `git reset --merge` for a stash conflict), and the finish button (`FinishMerge`, enabled only once every conflict is resolved).
- **Conflicts and MERGED BY ID**: groups `sceneformat.MergeResult.Conflicts` object by object under **CONFLICTS**, each row showing a status dot, object name, `Unresolved` / `Agent` / `Mine` / `Theirs` (or `Upstream` / `Picked` / `Revert` / `Stash`) / `Custom` chip, and a plain-language summary (such as `Both moved it: x 560 here, x 600 on main` or `Your speed change; main deleted it`). Objects joined cleanly without conflicts appear under **MERGED BY ID** with which side added or changed them. **Take the agent's suggestion for all N** (`TakeAllAgentMergeSuggestions`) resolves every conflict in one click.
- **MINE, THEIRS, and RESULT previews**: displays clickable **MINE** (or **MINE (COMMIT BEING REPLAYED)** during rebase, with branch, author, short commit SHA) and **THEIRS** (**UPSTREAM** / **PICKED COMMIT** / **REVERT** / **STASH**) 2D scene preview canvases over a larger **RESULT** canvas. Unresolved objects render at both Mine and Theirs positions with dashed amber outlines so overlapping moves are immediately visible; selecting a resolution updates **RESULT** live.
- **Agent's suggestion, Mine, Theirs, and hand-edited YAML**: the right inspector shows three selectable cards for the focused object — **Agent's suggestion** (from `Editor.MergeSuggester` or deterministic `DefaultMergeSuggestion`, combining non-conflicting fields from both sides or explaining why a deletion or higher `z` holds), **Mine**, and **Theirs** (labeled by operation) — each with its YAML snippet and reason. **Edit the result YAML by hand** (`SetMergeCustomEditing`, `ApplyMergeCustomYAML`) validates custom object YAML with `KnownFields(true)` and `sceneformat.Validate` before applying it.
- **Verification and Git completion (`FinishMerge`)**: writes canonical `sceneformat.Encode` YAML to the scene path, runs `game check` (`runProjectCheck` or `Editor.MergeCheckRunner`) and a short 10-second deterministic playtest (`sceneformat.Build` + `Update` + `PhysicsScene.Step` or `Editor.MergePlaytestRunner`), refuses to finish if either fails, and otherwise stages the scene (`git add`) and completes the active operation (`git commit --no-edit` for merge, `git rebase --continue` for rebase, `git cherry-pick --continue` for cherry-pick, `git revert --continue` for revert, or stage-only without committing for stash), reloading the resolved scene into the editor.
- **MCP tools (`editormcp`)**: `merge_status`, `merge_resolve`, `merge_finish`, and `merge_abort` expose the active `operation` and let coding agents inspect conflicted objects, supply or refine suggestions and reasons, apply choices or custom YAML, and finish or abort merges, rebases, cherry-picks, reverts, or stash conflicts.

## Find usages and safe rename (#1041)

Pressing `Shift+F12` (`CmdFindUsages`), choosing **Project > Find Usages** from the menu bar or Command Palette, right-clicking a file in the file browser (**Find Usages** / **Rename Safely…**), right-clicking an instanced scene or prefab object in the scene tree (**Find Usages**), or calling `OpenUsagesScreen(path)` opens the full-window **Find usages and safe rename** screen (`ReferencesScreenView`, styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `References.dc.html`):

- **Grouped usages (`FindUsages`)**: shows **Back to the editor** (`Esc`), `"Usages of <file>"`, and `"N usages in M files"`, grouped into **SCENES** (each instanced object with its position and override count), **PREFAB VARIANTS** (files with `inherits:` targeting the file), **DATA TABLES** (rows referencing the path), and **GO CODE** (string literals and constants parsed from project `*.go` files via `go/parser` + `go/ast`, marked `not rewritten`). `↑`/`↓` moves through usages, clicking a scene card focuses that scene's instances on a plotted thumbnail grid, and `Enter` opens the scene with the referencing object selected and framed.
- **Safe rename review (`PreviewSafeRename` / `StartSafeRename` / `SetSafeRenameInput`)**: pressing **Rename…** (`F2`) shows a **NEW NAME** field between the fixed folder prefix and compound file suffix, validating live against `game mv` / `MoveFile` path rules (non-empty, safe relative path, free destination) and computing the exact dry-run rename plan without touching disk. The review lists the moved file (`"content unchanged"`), every rewritten YAML file with exact `−` (old) and `+` (new) line diffs showing preserved instance overrides, and every non-rewritten Go reference as a problem (`"<file> loads the old path and is not rewritten"`) with **Ask the agent to update it** (`AskAgentToUpdateUsage`, invoking `Editor.OnAskAgentUpdateRef`) and **Open `<file>`** (`Editor.OnOpenSourceFile`).
- **One-step Keep and Undo (`ApplySafeRename` / `UndoSafeRename`)**: clicking **Keep · Rename N files** (`Mod+Enter`) writes only YAML through `MoveFile` as one undoable `Editor.History` step and enters the done summary with **Undo · ⌘Z** and **Back to `<scene>`**. One Undo restores every moved and rewritten file on disk byte-for-byte along with all open tabs.
- **MCP tools (`editormcp`)**: `refs_find` returns the grouped usages across scenes, prefab variants, data tables, and Go code (optionally opening the screen), and `refactor_rename` prepares the same safe-rename review in the editor with exact planned diffs and non-rewritten Go warnings without silently mutating disk unless `apply: true` is passed.

## Material editor (#896)

Assign and edit shared `*.material.yaml` resource files ([resource](resources.md)) directly inside the editor with live preview and full Undo/Redo:

- **Assigning and creating materials**: in the Inspector, the searchable **Material** selector (`SetObjectMaterial`) picks from the open project's `*.material.yaml` files; **New Material** (`CreateObjectMaterial` / `CreateMaterialFile`) creates a new canonical `.material.yaml` file written with `resource.Encode`, assigns it to the selected object, and opens it; **Edit Material** (`OpenMaterial` / `OpenMaterialProjectFile`), double-clicking a material file or pressing **Open** / **Edit Material** in the file browser, picking a material in the Command Palette (`OpenProjectFile`), or invoking `Project > Material Editor` (`CmdMaterialEditor`) opens the docked `MaterialEditorPanelView` below the viewport.
- **Every material field**: edits `name` (`SetMaterialName`), `baseColor` and `emissive` via NSGo `ColorWell` / `ColorPicker` popups and CSS color inputs (`SetMaterialBaseColor`, `SetMaterialEmissive`), `texture` via a searchable project image picker (`SetMaterialTexture`, `ProjectTextures`), `metallic`, `roughness`, and `opacity` via bounded sliders and numeric inputs (`SetMaterialMetallic`, `SetMaterialRoughness`, `SetMaterialOpacity`), `blend` from `resource.BlendModes()` (`SetMaterialBlend`), and `doubleSided` / `unlit` checkboxes (`SetMaterialDoubleSided`, `SetMaterialUnlit`). Invalid values are refused by `resource.Encode` without mutating state or history.
- **Live preview and viewport sync**: the panel renders a live preview (`MaterialPreviewImage`, `SetMaterialPreviewShape`) as a 3D **Sphere** or **Cube** lit with `render3d` (respecting `unlit`, PBR metallic/roughness, emissive, texture, opacity, and double-sidedness) or a 2D **Sprite** preview (defaulting to Sprite in 2D view and Sphere in 3D view). Every edit—including color-picker drags and Undo/Redo—pushes the updated material live through `resource.Library.Reload` (using `project.Resolver.Library()` and `scenemodel.Assets.Library()`), updating the preview, every 2D sprite (`BuiltScene.SyncMaterials`), and every 3D viewport object using that material immediately before saving.
- **Undo/Redo and deterministic save**: every edit goes through `Editor.History` (`EditMaterial`), tracks unsaved state (`MaterialDirty`, `HasUnsavedChanges`), follows file renames (`MoveFile`), and **Save Material** (`SaveMaterial`, `SaveMaterialFile`, `Save`, `SaveAll`, or `Editor.OnSaveMaterial`) writes the exact deterministic YAML produced by `resource.Encode`.
## Playtest report screen (#1044)

Open the full-window **Playtest Report** screen (`PlaytestReportView`) from `Project > Playtest Report`, Command Palette (`mod+shift+y` / `CmdPlaytestReport`), the status-bar playtest badge, double-clicking any `*.report.yaml` file in the file browser, programmatically via `OpenPlaytestReport` / `OpenPlaytestReportFile`, or from an agent calling `playtest_report`. Styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` color and contrast tokens (including `#101216` text on `#3B7BFF` primary accent buttons) matching `design/editor/Playtest.dc.html`:

- **Header and replay hand-off**: displays `"Playtest · <scope>"`, the project name and runs summary (such as `"20 headless runs by the agent · 1 m 40 s"`), **Watch run N** (`WatchPlaytestRun` / `WatchPlaytestRunIn`, which loads that run's saved `.replay.yaml` file through `replay.LoadFile`, starts Play mode with a live `*replay.Player` via `PlayReplayIn`, and provides a **Back to Playtest** banner), and **Ask the agent to tune it** (`AskAgentToTunePlaytest` / `Editor.OnPlaytestPrompt`).
- **BOT PROFILE strip and `playtest_define` review**: shows the bot profile switcher/name (`BotProfileDisplayName`), `"playtest.yaml from template <id>"` with **Open** (`OpenPlaytestConfigPreview` / `Editor.OnOpenPlaytestConfig`), and a one-sentence strategy. When the agent proposes a metric through `playtest_define`, a violet banner (`"Proposed with playtest_define: …"`) appears with **Review**, opening an inline unified-diff drawer to **Apply** or **Dismiss** without touching `playtest.yaml` on disk until accepted (`ApplyPlaytestProposal`, `DismissPlaytestProposal`).
- **Metric cards**: displays each metric's label, a text-and-icon **Met** or **Missed** badge (never color alone), formatted value, foot line, and `"Goal · <goal>"` paired with its monospace source file (`brief.yaml`, `game.yaml`, or `playtest.yaml`).
- **Automatic chart selection (all 4 chart kinds)**: `SelectPlaytestChart` and `DrawPlaytestChart` choose and render one canvas chart per report from the report's metrics:
  1. **Heat map over the level** (`PlaytestChartHeatMap`) for deaths or time per tile, with clustered density blobs and hotspot callout chips.
  2. **Bars per wave against a goal line** (`PlaytestChartBars`) with a dashed target line and flagged standout bars.
  3. **Line per level** (`PlaytestChartLine`) for per-level progression curves with flagged spike/dip markers.
  4. **Win-rate matrix** (`PlaytestChartMatrix`) with cells above the threshold (default 60%) flagged by border, marker, and `"above 60%"` label.
- **Agent's read, actionable suggestions, and footnote**: presents a concise diagnostic read (`DefaultPlaytestAgentRead` or custom `SetPlaytestAgentRead`) and clickable suggestion cards (`RequestPlaytestSuggestion`) that dispatch requests coming back as reviews, above the footnote noting that runs use the replay system and that metrics and the bot come from `playtest.yaml`.
- **MCP integration (`editormcp`)**: `game_playtest` loads freshly generated reports into the editor, `playtest_define` pushes pending proposals directly onto the Playtest Report banner with synced Apply/Dismiss handlers, and `playtest_report` lets the agent open, inspect, annotate, or watch runs from the report screen.
## Asset drawer (#1052)

Switch the top of the left rail between **Scene** (`LeftRailScene`) and **Assets** (`LeftRailAssets`, also reachable via Command Palette `CmdToggleAssetsMode`) to browse, filter, preview, and drag every kind of project asset directly into the scene, styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` design tokens and laid out from `design/editor/Assets.dc.html` (screen 1):

- **Left rail (`AssetsLeftRailView`)**: switches between **Scene** and **Assets** (`LeftRailSwitchView`), lists project **FOLDERS** with live asset counts (including empty asset directories such as `audio/music`), provides **SHOW ONLY** filters with counts (**Unused**, **Missing files**, **Generated**, **Proposed**), and includes a file drop zone (`"Drop files anywhere to import"`). **Unused** and **Missing files** are computed directly from `refs.Project.UnreferencedAssets` and `refs.Project.MissingAssets`.
- **Drawer under the viewport (`AssetDrawerView`)**: offers kind tabs (**All**, **Sprites**, **Tiles**, **Audio**, **Fonts**), a live **Name or path** filter (`SetAssetDrawerQuery`), a sort toggle defaulting to recently changed (`SetAssetDrawerSort`), **Import** (`TriggerAssetImport` / `ImportAssetFiles`), and **Generate** (`TriggerAssetGenerate`). Cards display a checkerboard preview, a status badge (`new`, `Missing`, `Generated`, `Not used`), the asset name, and a meta line (such as `"6 frames · used 31×"`). The footer reports `"N assets · M shown"` and any missing-file problem (`"1 problem · door_open.png missing"`). Empty folders display `"audio/music is empty · Drop .ogg or .wav files here, or ask the agent."` and empty filter results display `"Clear a filter to see more assets."`.
- **Drag to viewport (place snapped or swap sprite)**: dragging a sprite card onto empty space in the 2D viewport shows a snapped drop ghost and pill (`"coin.png new Sprite on Actors · snapped to 16 px"`) and places a new sprite object on the active layer in **one undo step** (`DropAssetAt`). Dropping a sprite onto an existing object swaps that object's sprite in **one undo step**, so one **Undo** restores the previous sprite. Pressing **Escape** (`CancelAssetDrag`) cancels the drag cleanly without mutating the scene.
- **Selected asset panel (`AssetInspectorView`)**: displays a checkerboard preview with dimensions/format metadata, frame-by-frame playable clip controls (`SetAssetPreviewPlaying`, `SetAssetPreviewClip`, `SetAssetPreviewFrame`, `StepAssetPreviewFrame`) for multi-frame sprites, playable sound controls (`ToggleAssetAudioPreview`, `StopAssetAudioPreview`) with waveform preview for audio files, a compact **IMPORT** summary from `InspectAssetImport`, and **USED BY** listing every referring file and usage from `refs.Project.UsedBy` (clicking a reference opens it). Unused assets display `"Nothing references it. The agent can propose deleting it; deleting files always asks you."`, and missing assets offer **Replace with a file** (`TriggerReplaceMissingAsset` / `ReplaceMissingAsset`) and **Ask the agent** (`AskAgentAboutAsset`).

## Viewport Overlays menu and agent viewport images (#1040)

In 2D edit mode (`ViewMode2D`), a floating bar at the bottom-center of the viewport (`design/editor/Inspector.dc.html`, styled strictly with `Spec.dc.html` tokens) pairs zoom (`−` / `%` / `+`), **Snap**, and **Fit** with an **Overlays N** button showing the count of active overlays:

- **`SHOW IN VIEWPORT` popover (`ui.Popover`)**: opens above **Overlays N** (also reachable via `View > Overlays` and `CmdViewportOverlays`) with a swatch, localized label, live count in the current scene, and `ui.Switch` for each overlay, plus the footer note *"The agent's screenshots use the same set: `viewport_overlays`"*:
  - **Collision shapes** (`collision_shapes`): registered non-trigger component shape fields (#882) and non-trigger 2D physics colliders.
  - **Triggers** (`triggers`): trigger/sensor/area component shapes and 2D physics trigger volumes (amber dotted outline).
  - **Connections** (`connections`): arched arrows from emitting object to handler object labelled `"event → handler"` (such as `"pulled → open"`) from the scene's `connections`.
  - **Patrol paths** (`patrol_paths`): enemy/actor patrol ranges and waypoint paths, built on the same `ViewportOverlay` registry that plugins use.
  - **Navigation** (`navigation`): built 2D navigation mesh polygons, portals, and `navregion2d` outlines (#888), kept in sync with `ShowNavMesh2D`.
  - **Camera bounds** (`camera_bounds`): scene camera bounds rectangle and `"bounds W × H"` caption (#884).
  - **Labels** (`labels`): compact object name/ID pills below visible objects.
- **Custom plugin overlays (`ViewportOverlay`)**: plugins add custom overlays via `Plugin.Overlays` (or directly on an editor with `Editor.RegisterViewportOverlay`), supplying an `ID`, `Name`/`NameKey`, optional `Count` callback, and `Draw func(*Editor, *core.Drawing, *sceneformat.BuiltScene)` function. Active plugin overlays appear in the popover, `View > Overlays`, and `editormcp`'s `viewport_image`.
- **Per-user persistence**: toggling any overlay (`SetViewportOverlay`, `ToggleViewportOverlay`, `SetViewportOverlays`) writes `viewportOverlays` to `Editor.SettingsPath` (`EditorSettings` YAML outside the project), never to any project or scene file, so choices survive restarts (including an explicit empty list `[]` when all overlays are turned off).
- **Shared with `editormcp`'s `viewport_image`**: `Editor.DrawViewportWithOverlays(d, size, overlayIDs)` renders the exact same overlay set into `viewport_image`. Omitting `viewport_overlays` uses the editor's active overlays; passing explicit names (or `[]`) overrides them for that image without mutating editor settings.

## Inspector titled sections, field filter, scrub fields, Open YAML, and Ask to add (#1038)

The object Inspector (`InspectorView`), styled with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `Inspector.dc.html`, `Main.dc.html`, and `Viewport3D.dc.html`, organizes object editing into compact collapsible sections while keeping every control backed by an `Editor` method and matching `editormcp` tool:

- **Header and Open YAML**: displays a kind-colored swatch, object ID, kind-and-layer subtitle (such as `Sprite · layer actors`), and **Open YAML** (`FindObjectYAMLLine` / `OpenSelectedObjectYAML`), which locates the object's exact 1-based line in the scene YAML (reading the file on disk when clean so comments and formatting match, or `SaveYAML` when dirty), invokes `Editor.OnOpenFileAtLine(path, line)` when set, and reports `Opened <path>:<line>` in the status bar.
- **Field filter (`InspectorFilter` / `SetInspectorFilter`)**: the **Filter fields** search bar narrows visible sections and fields by case-insensitive substring match against field labels, component names, and section titles (for example, filtering `speed` leaves only fields matching `speed`), auto-expanding matching sections and hiding non-matching ones.
- **Titled collapsible sections and compact transform rows**: sections render in Spec section-header style (uppercase title, chevron toggle via `ToggleInspectorSection`, field count badge, e.g. `TRANSFORM`, `SPRITE`, `PATROL`, `HEALTH`), with Position and Scale laid out as compact `X Y` rows (plus `Z` in 3D, alongside 3D Rotation `X Y Z`), Alpha, and Z order.
- **Scrubbable number fields**: dragging any number field's label (`BeginInspectorScrub`, `UpdateInspectorScrub`, `EndInspectorScrub`, `CancelInspectorScrub`) previews changes live in the viewport and records **one undo step** per drag; pressing `Escape` during a scrub cancels and restores the original value without touching history. In 3D view, holding `Shift` while scrubbing steps in `0.01` increments. Focused numeric fields support `↑`/`↓` stepping, `Shift` ×10 stepping, `Enter` commit, and `Escape` restore.
- **Grouped Add component and Ask to add**: registered components the object lacks are grouped under `GAMEPLAY`, `PHYSICS`, and `AUDIO AND VISUAL` (`ComponentCategoryOf`, `AddableComponentGroups`). Beside the input, **Ask to add** (`AskToAddComponent`, `ComposerDraft`, `SetComposerDraft`, `Editor.OnComposerPrompt`) stages `"Add a component to <object> that "` in the agent composer.
- **Review and problem indicators plus footer**: fields with active agent review changes (`SetInspectorReviewChanges`) show an amber accent bar and `"was <old> · in review"`; fields failing a project check or schema bound (`SetInspectorFieldProblems` / `Editor.Problems`) show a problem pill beside the control and on the section header. The footer reminds authors: `"Everything else about this object is in its YAML. Ask the agent, or open the file."`
- **Matching MCP tools (`editormcp`)**: `object_set_field` edits any single object field, component field, or custom property through the same `Editor` commands as one undo step; `object_locate_yaml` returns an object's 1-based YAML line and snippet (optionally triggering `OnOpenFileAtLine`); and `scene_edit` `set` supports all 2D/3D inspector fields including `particles`, `model`, `gridMap`, `occluder`, `light`, and `camera`.

## Generated assets through MCP generator servers (#1053)

Open the full-window **Generated Assets** screen (`AssetGeneratorView`) from `Project > Generated Assets` or the Command Palette (`CmdGeneratedAssets` / `SetAssetGeneratorOpen`). Styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching Screen 2 of `design/editor/Assets.dc.html`, it connects to external MCP image and sound generator servers, checks returned candidates against the game's `brief.yaml`, and keeps generated output off disk until a person explicitly picks a candidate:

- **MCP generator servers (`GENERATORS · MCP`)**: configure servers (`GeneratorServerConfig`, `SetGeneratorServerConfigs`) under **Settings › Tools** (`EditorSettingsDialogView`) and connect via `ConnectAssetGenerator` (any `mcp.Transport`, mirroring `ConnectRemote`) or `ConnectAssetGeneratorCommand` (stdio process on desktop). `AssetGenerators` lists every connected or configured server (`images` or `sound`) with live connection status.
- **Untrusted output decoded in memory only**: `GenerateAsset` strictly decodes image payloads to raster pixels in memory (re-encoding clean PNG bytes, enforcing bounds, and refusing SVG/HTML/non-image payloads) and validates WAV (`audioconv.DecodeWAV`) or OGG audio in memory. Nothing is written to disk before **Pick**.
- **Preview backdrops, `brief.yaml` style checks, and sound audition**: image candidates preview side-by-side with their reference asset on selectable backdrops (`PREVIEW ON`: Floor 3, Floor 1, Checker via `SetAssetGenPreviewBackground`) with pass/fail checks for **Palette**, **Pixel grid**, **Size** against the reference asset, and **Contrast** against the selected wall (`LoadAssetStyleBrief`, `SetAssetStyleBrief`). Sound candidates display waveform bars, duration, RMS loudness (`dBFS`), peak loudness check against the reference sound or brief target, and in-memory audition (`PlayGeneratedSoundCandidate`, `StopGeneratedSoundCandidate`).
- **Atomic undoable Pick and provenance sidecar (`<file>.source.yaml`)**: **Pick** (`PickGeneratedCandidate`) writes **exactly** the picked media file and its deterministic version-1 `<file>.source.yaml` sidecar (`importsettings.AssetSource`, `AssetSourceVersion = 1`, schema `https://game.nsgo.dev/schemas/source.v1.json`) recording `version`, `generator`, `model`, `prompt`, `seed`, `references`, `license`, `commercialUse`, and `approvedBy` in **one undoable `Editor.History` step**; one `Editor.Undo()` removes both files cleanly. **Vary this** (`VaryGeneratedCandidate`), **Refine** (`RefineGeneratedAsset`), and **Regenerate N** (`RegenerateAsset`) re-invoke the generator with the same references without writing to disk.
- **Export license gate (`LICENSES · CHECKED AT EXPORT`)**: `credits.ScanGeneratedLicenses`, `credits.CheckGeneratedLicenses`, `credits.Collect`, `releaseplan.Build`, `game release`, and the editor's Export panel (`CheckExportCredits`) scan every `*.source.yaml` sidecar in the project. Any generated file with an empty or unknown license (`license: ""` or `license: unknown`) blocks release builds with `*credits.UnknownLicenseError` naming the asset's path until resolved (for example via `SetGeneratedAssetLicense` / **Set license**).
## Focus ring and keyboard regions (#1003)

Every interactive control in the editor carries the `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` focus ring on keyboard focus and is reachable via `Tab`, `F6`, or arrows:

- **Keyboard-only focus ring (`:focus-visible`)**: `EditorView` installs `theme.Core()` (also returned by `FocusTheme()`), mapping `info`, `focus`, and `ring` to Spec accent (`#3B7BFF` in Dark, `#1D5CE6` in Light) while preserving Spec Dark and Spec Light surfaces. Buttons, toggle buttons, selects, checkboxes, and `SpecIconButton` / `SpecSwitch` draw a 2 px accent ring with a +2 px offset on keyboard focus only—mouse clicks never draw it.
- **Inset rings and `:focus-within`**: tree rows (`SceneTreeView`, `LayersView`), list rows (`AssetBrowserView`, `FilesView`), text fields (`ui.Input`, `ui.NumberInput`), and F6-focused regions draw an inset `-2 px` accent ring via `theme.Palette.FocusRing(-2)` so scrolling parents never clip them. Selected and focused rows keep both their selection fill and the inset ring. Composer and card containers (`SpecFocusWithinCard`, `hud-composer-card`) draw the +2 px accent ring on the card via `:focus-within`.
- **Fixed-order keyboard regions (`F6` / `Shift-F6`)**: `F6` moves to the next visible region and `Shift-F6` moves to the previous region in fixed Spec order: **Header** (`RegionHeader`) → **Scene tree** (`RegionSceneTree`) → **Viewport** (`RegionViewport`) → **Agent and Inspector** (`RegionInspector`) → **Status bar** (`RegionStatusBar`) → **Toast** (`RegionToast`, included only while a toast is active in `ToastView` via `Editor.ShowToast`). Entering a region via `F6` / `Shift-F6` draws its inset `-2 px` ring and restores the last control focused in that region. `F6` / `Shift-F6` are suppressed during shortcut capture and full-window screens, and close any open object context menu.
- **Viewport focus is the selection**: the viewport canvas draws no focus ring of its own; while focus is in the viewport (`editor-viewport-frame`), `Tab` (`Editor.SelectNextObject`) and `Shift-Tab` (`Editor.SelectPreviousObject`) cycle through visible, unlocked scene objects in order.
- **Dialog focus trapping and opener restoration**: editor dialogs (`EditorSettingsDialogView`, `ShortcutsSheetView`, `CommandPaletteView`, unsaved-changes prompt, scene path dialog, and save-conflict dialog) trap `Tab` / `Shift-Tab` inside the topmost modal (respecting shortcut capture in `Settings › Shortcuts`), block global shortcuts behind the modal except `⌘.` / `Ctrl+.` (`Editor.OnStopAgent`) to stop an active agent, and return focus to the exact control that opened the dialog when dismissed with `Esc` or an action button.
- **Focus stability**: nothing moves focus automatically during an active drag or inspector scrub (`Editor.IsDragging`) or while typing in a text field (`Editor.IsTyping`). Showing a toast never steals focus, and `Editor.ShouldFocusAgentPrompt` returns `true` only when no drag or text input is active and focus is already inside the agent panel (`Editor.SetAgentPanelRef`).
## Connect an agent and status chip (#1009)

Connect an external coding agent (Claude Code, Codex, or any MCP client on your computer) directly from the editor or with `go tool game mcp install`, matching `design/editor/Connect.dc.html`, `design/editor/AgentStates.dc.html`, and `design/editor/Settings.dc.html` using exclusively `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens:

- **One-time project install (`go tool game mcp install`)**: writes `.mcp.json` (Claude Code) and `.codex/config.toml` (Codex) idempotently in the project root while preserving existing unrelated servers and settings, and prints each file written or left unchanged.
- **Connect dialog (`ConnectAgentDialogView`)**: reachable from `Project > Connect an Agent...`, Command Palette (`CmdConnectAgent`), `Editor Settings > Models and connections` (`+ Connect another agent or model`), or the Agent panel header. Offers three choices (**An agent on this computer** — Recommended, **Built in, with your API key**, and **No agent**), shows `go tool game mcp install` with a **Copy** button, displays `"Waiting for an agent to connect…"` which turns to `"Connected · <agent> over <transport>"` as soon as the first MCP client attaches, provides four starting permission checkboxes (**Edit scenes and data (always reviewable)**, **Run game check**, **Run headless playtests**, **Edit Go code** -> `AgentPermissionRules`), keeps the Agent panel closed when **No agent** is chosen, and provides **Later** and **Done**.
- **Agent tab panel (`AgentPanelView`)**: switches the right sidebar between **Inspector** (`RightTabInspector`) and **Agent** (`RightTabAgent`, `CmdAgentPanel`), showing the thread of requests (including turns made by terminal-started MCP clients), streamed steps, and a labelled multi-line composer (`Enter` sends, `Shift+Enter` adds a line, plus **Send** / **Stop**). Composer requests run through `Editor.AgentRunner` (defaulting on desktop to `CLIAgentRunner`, invoking Claude Code's `--print --output-format=stream-json --verbose` or Codex's `codex exec --json` with the project's MCP configuration).
- **Status chip (`AgentStatusChipView`)**: announces transitions through `core.LiveRegionPolite` and renders all five states while keeping manual tools working in every state:
  - `Connected · MCP` (hover tooltip names the agent and transport, such as `Claude Code over stdio`)
  - `Working · step n of m` (violet dot pulses on a `1.2 s` cycle, staying still when `ReduceMotion` is enabled)
  - `Waiting for you · m:ss` (clicking focuses the open request card)
  - `Reconnecting · try n of 5` (retries after `1, 2, 4, 8, 16 s` using injectable `Editor.AgentScheduleAfter`)
  - `Disconnected` with **Reconnect** and **Troubleshoot** (reopens Connect directly at the failed step rather than the start)
  - `Disconnected · offline` when the editor is offline.

## Play mode marks, rolling replay, and frozen-frame agent questions (#1042)

While playing a scene in Play mode (`Play`, `PlayIn`, `StepPlay`, `PlayTick`) or connected to a running game, the editor keeps a rolling 60-second input recording, supports marking moments without stopping, lets you freeze any frame to ask the agent what looks wrong, and adds panic triage with one-click replay up to the panic frame (`design/editor/PlayMode.dc.html`, styled strictly with `Spec.dc.html` tokens):

- **Rolling 60-second input recording**: every fixed Play step samples `Editor.PlayInputSource` (watching `Editor.PlayInputActions` / `Editor.PlayInputAxes`, defaulting to the project's input actions in `game.yaml`) into a bounded 60-second sliding buffer (`60 × PlayRateHz()`, 3,600 frames at 60 Hz; `PlayRollingFrameCount`, `PlayRollingSummary` showing `"Last 60 s kept · N frames"`). Marking, asking, or hitting a panic atomically writes a version 2 `replay` YAML file under `replays/<scene>-<frame>.replay.yaml` (`SavePlayRollingReplay`, `PlayLastReplayPath`) that rebases older windows cleanly while preserving held actions and axis values entering the window.
- **Play mode bar and marks (`alt+f` / `CmdPlayMark`)**: local Play mode renders a Spec-styled Play control bar (`playModeBarView`, keeping the 2D edit toolbar compact) with live/paused status pill, Pause/Resume (`mod+shift+p`, `SetPlayPaused`), single-frame **Step** (`.` while paused, `StepPlay`), time scale (`0.25×`–`2×`, `SetPlayTimeScale`), **Mark `⌥F`** (`AddPlayMark`), **Something looks wrong `⌘L`**, and **Stop**. Pressing `alt+f` marks the current frame without stopping Play, saves its `.replay.yaml` under `replays/`, pins a flag on the 60-second frame-time strip, and lists it under **MARKS · N** in the Inspector's **Agent** tab (`PlayInspectorTab`, `PlayMarks`) with time, frame, and `"replay attached"`. Stopping Play (`Stop`)—or clicking **Send marks now** (`SendPlayMarks`) earlier—bundles all unsent marks into **one thread** dispatched to `Editor.OnPlayMarksThread` and stored in `LastPlayMarksThread`.
- **Frozen-frame questions (`mod+l` / `CmdAskFrozenFrame`) and MCP replay stepping**: pressing `mod+l` or **Something looks wrong** (`OpenAskFrozenFrame`) pauses Play, captures the frozen viewport PNG, and opens **"Ask about frame N"** with the frame preview, attachment chips, prompt field (`"What looks wrong? e.g. the slime walks through the wall"`), `"A fix comes back as a review"`, **Cancel**, and **Ask**. Submitting (`SubmitAskFrozenFrame`) saves the rolling replay, attaches the frame image and live entity snapshot, dispatches `Editor.OnAskFrozenFrame`, and switches to the **Agent** tab. Connected agents inspect the frozen frame through `editormcp`'s `play_frozen_context` tool and step the replay through `play_replay_step` (`AgentStepPlayReplay`), showing the viewport agent pill (`"agent stepping frames A–B"`) and step log (`"Stepped frames A–B with game_loop_step…"`) while keeping the game paused until the person resumes.
- **Output panel panic card and Replay up to the panic**: `ParsePanicTrace` detects Go panic messages and stack traces in run output or recovered Play panics (`OutputPanic`, `SetOutputPanic`). `OutputPanelView` displays a Spec-styled panic card with the message, clickable `file:line` links for game stack frames, folded runtime frames (`"N frames in the game runtime · Show"` / `"Hide"`, `SetOutputPanicRuntimeExpanded`), **Ask the agent to fix** (`AskAgentToFixPanic`, `Editor.OnAskPanicFix`), and **Replay up to the panic** (`ReplayUpToPanic`, `ReplayUpToFrame`), which resets the scene (calling `Editor.OnPlayReset` when set), replays the saved recording through `PlayActiveInput()`, and stops and pauses at `panicFrame - 1` without triggering the panic.

## Permissions and MCP enforcement (#1010)

The editor governs every mutating and running MCP tool call with a tracked, versioned YAML rules file, `permissions.yaml`, stored beside `game.yaml` (keeping `.nsgo/` git-ignored for editor-local state while teams share and review permission rules in Git):

- **Three profile presets**:
  - **Cautious** (`cautious`): asks before every content edit, config edit, code edit, file creation, `game check`, and playtest, and denies deleting files or running shell commands.
  - **Balanced** (`balanced`, default when `permissions.yaml` is absent): edits content (`scenes/**`, `prefabs/**`, `data/**`) and runs `game check` and playtests on its own; asks before `game.yaml`, `brief.yaml`, `**/*.go`, and creating files anywhere; denies deleting any file or running shell commands.
  - **Autonomous** (`autonomous`): lets the agent complete whole tasks end to end while keeping reviewable undo steps; deletes and shell commands remain denied unless explicitly relaxed.
- **First-match-wins rule table**: each rule matches an action (`edit`, `create`, `delete`, `run`, `code`) and slash-separated file globs (`scenes/**`, `**/*.go`, `**`) or run targets (`game check`, `playtests`, `shell`) to a decision (`allow`, `ask`, `deny`). Every evaluation (`Editor.EvaluatePermission`) records the exact rule index or profile that decided it.
- **Settings › Permissions (`PermissionsSettingsView`)**: reachable via `File > Permissions...`, the Command Palette (`CmdPermissionsSettings`), or a blocked step link. Displays the three profile cards (retaining the active profile's name with a **Changed** badge when rules are edited from the preset), the reorderable rule table with an arrow-key-navigable **Allow / Ask / Deny** radio group per row, add/remove controls, and an **Agent on this page** rail with blocked-step links and pending approval cards.
- **Blocked step links and Always toast**: a `deny` rule never invokes the MCP tool and records a blocked step whose link (such as `"Settings › Permissions · rule 7 of 7"`) opens **Settings › Permissions** with the matching rule focused. Answering **Always** on an `ask` card inserts an `allow` rule ahead of the asking rule, saves `permissions.yaml`, and shows the `"Always permission rule saved"` toast with **Show** and **Undo**.
- **MCP-only scope**: rules govern the editor's MCP server tools only. External agent edits directly on disk continue to surface via the editor's disk watcher.
## Ask the agent in place (`⌘L`) and Agent panel (#1037)

Pressing `⌘L` (`Ctrl+L` on Windows and Linux, registered as `CmdAskAgent` in `ScopeGlobal` with `core.KeyNotEditing`) outside any text field opens an anchored in-place composer (`AskInPlaceComposerView`, styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `AskInPlace.dc.html`) directly beside the selected object(s) in the viewport:

- **Anchored placement and clamping (`OpenAskInPlace`, `AskInPlacePlacement`, `ComputeAskInPlacePlacement`)**: anchors below the selection's viewport bounds (flipping above when near the bottom edge) and clamps strictly inside the viewport container so it stays on screen and never overlaps the right panel (Inspector / Agent panel). Without a selection, it anchors inside the viewport asking about the open scene. Pressing `Esc` (`CloseAskInPlace`) closes without sending and restores focus to the control that held it before `⌘L`. Pressing `⌘L` while typing in the Inspector or any text input is ignored.
- **Announced selection chip, multi-line field, and contextual suggestions**: displays an accessible `RoleStatus` / `LiveRegionPolite` chip announcing the selection count and object names (for example `"2 selected · slime_01, slime_02"`), a real labelled multi-line input (`⇧↵` inserts a newline, `↵` or `⌘↵` sends), up to two contextual suggestions (such as `"Make them a prefab variant"` and `"Space them evenly"` for multi-selection) navigable with `↑`/`↓` and `↵` without blocking free typing, and the localized hint `"⌘L asks about the selection · ⌘K searches · right-click 'Ask the agent'"`.
- **Context menu wiring**: choosing **Ask the agent about this** (`AskAgentAboutObject`) from either the viewport context menu or the Scene Tree context menu opens the same anchored composer for the target object (or the full multi-selection when the object is part of one).
- **Agent panel and MCP context delivery**: sending starts an `AgentTurn` (`SubmitAskInPlace`) flagged `SkipPlan: true` (matching `Plan.dc.html`: *"Small edits with ⌘L skip the plan."*), attaches the selection's object IDs and names, the open scene path and YAML, and a rendered viewport PNG (`RenderViewportPNG`), switches the right rail (`RightRailView` / `RightRailTab`, toggleable with `⌘1` / `⌘2`) to the **Agent** tab (`AgentPanelView`) showing the turn with context chips, and notifies connected MCP clients through `editormcp`'s `editor://agent/turn` resource and `agent_turn_get` / `agent_turn_reply` tools.
## Background task list with queued and parallel agent requests (#1022)

Requests, plan steps, and automations share one background task list in the Agent panel (`TasksPanelView`, styled strictly with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `Tasks.dc.html`), toggled from the status bar indicator (`status-tasks`), `View > Background Tasks`, or `CmdToggleTasks` (`mod+shift+t`):

- **Task model and plain-text thread persistence (`AgentTask`, `AgentThreadDoc`)**: tasks move across five visible groups—**Needs you** (`TaskNeedsYou`), **Running** (`TaskRunning`), **Ready to review** (`TaskReadyToReview`), **Queued** (`TaskQueued`), and **Failed** (`TaskFailed`)—plus completed (`TaskDone`) and stopped/reverted (`TaskStopped`) states. Each project persists its task list in plain-text YAML under `.nsgo/threads/<id>.thread.yaml` (`AgentThreadVersion = 1`, deterministic output, unknown fields and duplicate keys refused via `ParseAgentThread` / `EncodeAgentThread`, round-trip lossless).
- **Queue vs Run in parallel (`SubmitTask`)**:
  - **Queue** (`TaskStartQueue`): waits behind the running or unreviewed turn and starts **only after** that turn is kept (`KeepTask`) or reverted (`RevertTask`), so queued requests always build on the reviewed scene.
  - **Run in parallel** (`TaskStartParallel`): starts immediately as a separate agent session with its own numbered overlay (`1`, `2`, …).
  - **Default runner and Connect guard**: when `Editor.AgentTaskRunner` is nil, `SubmitTask`, `SubmitComposerTask`, `tasks_submit`, and `advanceTaskQueue` run each task through the same agent route as the agent composer (`resolveRouteAndRunner` — external CLI or built-in API key runner) in a fresh session with its own `AgentTurn`, streaming steps, `Needs you` permission asks, `Ready to review`, and `Failed` transitions. Submitting a runnable task when no agent is connected opens the Connect dialog and creates no task.
- **Object holds and permissions in `editormcp` (`Server.ConnectTask`, `CompleteTaskTurn`, `ReleaseTaskHolds`, `TaskReviewSnapshot`)**: each parallel task runs on its own MCP session bound to its task ID (automatically via `NSGO_EDITOR_TASK_ID` for CLI subprocesses through `go tool game mcp` and via `WithTaskToolContext` for the built-in runner). Editing an object (via `scene_edit`, `object_set_field`, object menu tools, or tile edit tools) holds that object for the task's active turn; another parallel session trying to edit the same object holds cleanly in FIFO order, updates its step and overlay to `"waiting on <object>"` naming the holding task, and automatically resumes once the first task finishes its turn or releases holds—producing separate `TaskReview` entries per turn. Tools matching an `Ask` permission rule pause the task in **Needs you** (`BeginTaskPermissionAsk`) until the person's **Allow once** or **Deny** (`ResolveTaskPermission`) resolves it.
- **Numbered overlays in the viewport and scene tree**: active tasks draw solid violet (`theme.Palette.Agent`) outlines with numbered pills (`1 placing the jump arc`) on objects they are editing and 45%-opacity dashed outlines with a clock glyph and `"2 waiting on slime_02"` pill on objects they are holding, paired with a compact bottom-left viewport legend and `"1 · editing"` / `"2 · holding"` badges on scene tree rows (`ObjectTaskBadge`, `SceneTaskOverlays`).
- **Bulk dismiss and summary badge counts**: multiple **Ready to review** tasks can be checked (`ToggleTaskDismissSelection`) and dismissed together (`DismissSelectedTasks` / `DismissTasks`) without reverting anything that was kept. `TaskSummary` and `TaskDockBadgeCount` expose counts for `NeedsYou`, `Running`, `Blocked`, `ReadyToReview`, `Queued`, `Failed`, `MostUrgent`, and `DockBadge = NeedsYou + ReadyToReview + Failed` (excluding running and queued) for both the status bar (`No tasks`, `1 running · 0:38`, urgent-first mixed counts, persistent `Failed`) and downstream notifications/Dock badges (#1023).
- **Matching MCP tools (`editormcp`)**: `tasks_list` lists tasks, active holds, and summary counts; `tasks_submit` queues or starts parallel requests; and `tasks_control` runs `stop`, `retry`, `open`, and `focus`, `revert` of the calling session's own task, and `dismiss` of Failed tasks.
- **Person-only decisions**: keeping a task (`KeepTask`), answering its permission prompt (`ResolveTaskPermission`, allow or deny), and dismissing a task that is Ready to review are the person's decisions. Called by an agent (`WithAgentActor`, which every MCP tool call runs under), `KeepTask` and `ResolveTaskPermission` return `ErrTaskDecisionYouOnly` (`ErrAutomationYouOnly` for an automation task's keep), `DismissTasks` skips Ready to review tasks, and `tasks_control` refuses `keep`, `allow`, `deny`, `revert` of a task bound to another session, and `dismiss` of a Ready to review task.
## Game brief and project rules (#1026)

Settings (`EditorSettingsDialogView`, matching `design/editor/Settings.dc.html` and styled exclusively with `Spec.dc.html` tokens) adds two shared project pages under **AGENT** that the agent reads before every request:

- **Game brief (`brief.yaml`, `OpenBriefSettings`)**: edits the versioned `brief.yaml` file at the project root (`project.Brief`, `project.ParseBrief`, `project.EncodeBrief`, JSON Schema `https://game.nsgo.dev/schemas/brief.v1.json`) with labelled fields for **Pitch**, **Genre**, **Tags**, **Platforms**, **Art direction**, **Feel and difficulty** (including structured playtest targets readable via `playtest.TargetsFromBrief` / `playtest.ApplyBriefTargets` / `Editor.BriefPlaytestTargets`), and **Tone and audio**. Projects without `brief.yaml` show an empty state; filling and saving writes a canonical `brief.yaml` that round-trips byte-for-byte unchanged, unknown fields are refused with their 1-based line number (`loaderr`), missing sections appear as `"1 gap"` / `"N gaps"` in the sidebar, and every edit records an undoable step in `Editor.History` (`EditBrief`).
- **Project rules (`AGENTS.md`, `OpenRulesSettings`)**: edits the plain Markdown `AGENTS.md` file at the project root (shared by Claude Code, Codex, and the built-in agent) in a monospace code field as undoable steps (`SetProjectRules`), showing the bulleted rule count (`ProjectRulesCount`) in navigation. Patterns of consistently reverted reviews (`RecordReviewOutcome` / `SuggestedProjectRules`) surface suggested rules (such as `"Suggested from your last 30 reviews: you reverted every change that moved the camera bounds."`); pressing **Add as a rule** (`ProposeSuggestedRule`) opens a reviewable diff card (**Keep** / **Revert**) without modifying `AGENTS.md` on disk until kept.
- **Reviewable agent edits**: agent proposals through `editormcp`'s `brief_edit` and `rules_edit` queue pending `DocReview` cards (`ProposeBriefReview` / `ProposeProjectRulesReview`, `KeepDocReview` / `RevertDocReview`) without touching disk until kept; kept reviews apply as one undoable step in `Editor.History`.
- **Agent on this page and Settings search**: both Agent pages render the right-hand **Agent on this page** card with a contextual note, clickable suggested asks, and an inline composer; clicking a suggested ask or submitting a prompt in the top Settings search bar calls `AskAgentFromSettings`, staging the page-contextualized prompt in `ComposerDraft` and invoking `Editor.OnSettingsAgentRequest` (and `Editor.OnComposerPrompt`).

## History panel: your edits and the agent's turns on one timeline (#1017)

The History panel (`HistoryPanelView`), styled with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `AskInPlace.dc.html` and `Checkpoints.dc.html`, brings manual edits, kept agent turns, external Git reloads, and saved points onto one chronological timeline:

- **Rich timeline entries (`History.Entries`, `Editor.HistoryEntries`)**: each `HistoryEntry` records who made the change (`HistoryAuthorYou`, `HistoryAuthorAgent`, or `HistoryAuthorGit`), a concise headline and detail (such as `You · moved Knight` / `x 416 → 452` or `Agent · slimes and coins` / `6 changes · kept 5`), the list of touched files, change/kept counts, and a timestamp driven by an injectable clock (`Editor.SetHistoryClock` / `History.SetClock`) for deterministic testing. Saving records a `HistoryEntrySaved` marker (`Saved · level_01.scene.yaml`) capturing the saved snapshot.
- **Unified multi-file agent turns (`Editor.RecordAgentTurn`)**: groups scene, project, and extra touched project files under one undoable `History` step tagged as `HistoryAuthorAgent`, alongside automatic agent attribution for `editormcp` edits (`EnterAgentContext` / `LeaveAgentContext`), `KeepTileReview`, and `KeepCutsceneReview`.
- **Opening and filtering**: press `⌘Y` (`Ctrl+Y` on Windows/Linux, `CmdHistoryPanel`), choose **View > History**, or click the toolbar history icon button (tooltip `"History · ⌘Y"`). Entries appear newest-first grouped by day (`TODAY`, `YESTERDAY`, or date) below a **Now** row marked `unsaved` when dirty, with **All** / **You** / **Agent** filter tabs (`Editor.SetHistoryFilter`).
- **Read-only point-in-time preview (`PreviewHistoryEntry`, `ExitHistoryPreview`)**: clicking any timeline entry renders that step's snapshot read-only in the 2D and 3D viewports with a `"Previewing <relative time> · editing is off"` banner, without mutating `Editor.Doc` or moving `History`'s undo cursor. Pressing `Esc`, **Back**, or **Now** exits preview cleanly.
- **Non-destructive Restore (`RestoreHistoryEntry`)**: clicking **Restore** rolls the active scene, project, and every extra touched file back to that point as **one new undoable step** in `History`. Later steps remain in the timeline and can still be previewed or restored, and a single `Undo` returns immediately to the latest state.
- **Agent read access (`editormcp`)**: the read-only `history_list` tool (`ActionRead`) exposes the same newest-first timeline to connected agents with optional `author` (`all`, `you`, `agent`) and `limit` filters plus unsaved and active preview metadata.

## Persistent per-turn checkpoints (#1018)

Agent turns, autonomous tasks, and automations persist across editor restarts inside the project's `.nsgo/checkpoints/` directory and appear alongside session edits in the unified History panel (`⌘Y`, `CmdHistoryPanel`):

- **Lazy first-write capture (`.nsgo/checkpoints/`)**: before an agent turn (`BeginCheckpointTurn` / `RunCheckpointTurn`, or any file-mutating `editormcp` `ActionEdit` tool call) first modifies a project file—scenes, prefabs, data tables, `game.yaml`, Go code, or verbatim binary media—the editor snapshots its pre-turn bytes under `.nsgo/checkpoints/<id>/files/<path>` and writes a strict versioned YAML manifest (`manifest.yaml`, `editor.CheckpointVersion = 1`, JSON Schema `https://game.nsgo.dev/schemas/checkpoint.v1.json`). Files created by the turn record `existed: false` so restoring deletes them. `.nsgo/checkpoints/.gitignore` and `.nsgo/.gitignore` are written automatically so checkpoints stay ignored by Git, and `Editor.CheckpointMaxBytes` (default `128 MiB`) prunes the oldest completed checkpoints when total size exceeds the cap.
- **Tasks and automations**: autonomous background tasks (`TakeCheckpointBeforeTask`, `RunTaskWithCheckpoint`, and `SubmitTask` runners) and automations (`TakeCheckpointBeforeAutomation`, `RunAutomationWithCheckpoint`, `game_playtest`, and automation-mode MCP edits) always persist a checkpoint before they start.
- **Security and integrity**: checkpoint capture, writes, and restores strictly exclude `.git/`, `.nsgo/`, and all You-only configuration files (`MatchYouOnlyPath` / `CheckYouOnlyTarget`), refuse symlinks along every path component, cap manifest and file sizes (`2 MiB` / `32 MiB`), and verify both byte length and SHA-256 digests before writing.
- **Unified History preview and one-step undoable restore**: `OpenProject` reloads persisted checkpoints into `HistoryPanelView` (`UnifiedHistoryEntries`). Clicking a checkpoint previews its scene snapshot read-only (`PreviewCheckpoint`), and **Restore** (`RestoreCheckpoint`) atomically restores every touched file on disk, synchronizes open tabs and `game.yaml`, and records one undoable step (`checkpointRestoreCmd`) so `Undo` (`⌘Z`) puts the entire turn back byte-for-byte.
- **Matching MCP tools (`editormcp`)**: `checkpoint_list` (`ActionRead`) lists unified history and persisted checkpoints; `checkpoint_restore` (`ActionEdit`) evaluates permission rules (`edit`, `code`, `delete`) and You-only boundaries against every file recorded in the checkpoint manifest before restoring as one undoable step.

## You-only trust boundary and proposed deletions (#1015)

Settings that govern what an AI agent is allowed to touch — **Permissions** (`permissions.yaml`), **Tools** (`tools.yaml` / `mcp.yaml`), **Models** (`models.yaml`), and **Usage** (`usage.yaml`) — follow the **You-only rule** (`design/editor/Permissions.dc.html`, `Settings.dc.html`):

- **Hard MCP refusal (`editormcp`)**: Every non-read tool on the editor's MCP server refuses any write, rename (including bare-stem `refactor_rename`), or deletion targeting a You-only file (`permissions.yaml`, `.nsgo/permissions.yaml`, `tools.yaml`, `models.yaml`, `usage.yaml`, or per-user `editor.yaml`, including through symlinks and case-insensitive host aliases), returning a structured `*editormcp.YouOnlyError` even when the active profile is `Autonomous`.
- **Permissions draft card (`permissions_draft` / `PermissionsDraftCardView`)**: The agent can only submit a draft via `permissions_draft` (`Editor.SubmitPermissionsDraft`). The draft opens as its own modal `ui.Dialog` with focus trapping and opener focus restoration, showing each rule's `NOW → DRAFT` decision, a live `permissions.yaml · if applied` YAML diff preview, per-rule checkboxes with **nothing ticked by default**, **Discard draft**, and **Apply n rules**. `⌘↵`, **Keep all**, automations, and every profile (`Autonomous` included) cannot apply the draft. Applied rules are recorded in `History` as the person's (`HistoryActorPerson`) and can be undone byte-for-byte with `⌘Z`.
- **External disk changes to `permissions.yaml` (`ReconcileExternalPermissions`)**: When `permissions.yaml` changes on disk outside the editor (a clone, `git pull`, or another tool), **tightened profiles and rules apply at once**, while **loosened profiles or rules** (such as changing `Balanced` to `Autonomous`, turning `Deny` into `Allow`, or removing a `Deny` rule) are **held** and not enforced until the person explicitly confirms each entry in **Settings › Permissions** (`ConfirmHeldPermissionLoosening` / `RejectHeldPermissionLoosening`).
- **Proposed deletions that Keep all never applies (`game_delete_file` / `AgentReviewPanelView`)**: `game_delete_file` is off by default (`Editor.ToolEnabled("game_delete_file") == false`) and always proposes a deletion in the **Review changes** panel (`Editor.ProposeFileDeletion`) showing the file's exact line count (or binary summary), a red-tinted preview, and whether any scene uses it (via `Editor.FindUsages`), while refusing symlinks, `.git/**`, `game.yaml`, open scene tabs, and files above 1 MiB. **Keep all** (`Editor.KeepAllReviewChanges` / `⌘↵`) keeps the other changes and leaves pending deletion files untouched on disk; confirming in place (**Delete this file** / `Editor.ConfirmProposedDeletion`) deletes the file through a reversible checkpoint command (`deleteFileCmd`), and **`⌘Z` (`Editor.Undo`)** restores the file byte-for-byte.
## Toasts and feedback channels (#1004)

Passing feedback in the editor uses one Spec-styled toast component (`ToastView`, `WindowToastView`, and `SpecToastCard`, matching `design/editor/Toasts.dc.html` and `Spec.dc.html` using exclusively `theme.Palette` tokens). Decisions never go in a toast: permission asks and questions stay in Agent panel cards, check failures go to Problems, and finished agent turns open Review.

- **Five kinds on one 40 px, radius 12, single-line pill**:
  - `ToastSuccess` (check-in-circle icon, `Added.Fg`), `ToastInfo` (`i`-in-circle icon, `TextMuted`), `ToastWarning` (triangle icon, `Warning.Fg`, on `Warning.Fill` and `Warning.Edge`), `ToastError` (circle-with-bar icon, `Removed.Fg`), and `ToastProgress` (progress ring + bottom 2 px bar + live percent).
  - At most one verb action button (with its shortcut hint, such as `Undo ⌘Z`) and a dismiss button on every kind except `ToastProgress`.
- **Canonical Spec event API (`Editor.EmitToastEvent`, `ToastHandle`, `SpecFeedbackChannels`)**: screens emit typed `ToastEvent` values (`ToastEventReviewKept`, `ToastEventReviewReverted`, `ToastEventFixApplied`, `ToastEventAgentStopped`, `ToastEventPermissionSaved`, `ToastEventCopied`, `ToastEventDiskChanged`, `ToastEventSaveFailed`, `ToastEventExportRunning`, `ToastEventExportFailed`) instead of inventing ad-hoc tones, durations, or queue keys. `EmitToastEvent` returns a `*ToastHandle` so long-running work such as `export_panel.go` exports can update completion (`SetProgress`), transition in place into a success toast (`Complete`, e.g. `"Exported <path>"` with **Show in Finder**) or persistent error toast (`Fail` with **Retry**), or cancel the running work (`Cancel`).
- **Placement**: bottom center of the viewport, 8 px above the viewport bar (never over the scene tree, inspector, or agent panel). When no viewport is on screen (Start screen, Settings, Export, or other full-window views), `WindowToastView` anchors at the bottom center of the window, 24 px up.
- **Queue, waiting badge, merging, and ordering**: one toast is shown at a time; others wait in FIFO order and the visible toast displays a `+N` waiting count badge. Repeating the same event merges into the existing queued entry in place without changing order, and nothing jumps ahead of an active error or running progress toast.
- **Timing, pause, motion, and accessibility**:
  - `ToastDuration` enforces Spec lifetimes: success and info stay for 4 s (6 s with an action), warning stays for 8 s, and error and progress stay until retried, dismissed, canceled, or completed. Hover, touch, keyboard focus inside `RegionToast`, or the window leaving `core.LifecycleActive` (going to the background) pauses the countdown (`Editor.ToastTimerPaused`, `Editor.ToastRemaining`).
  - Enter motion fades in and rises 8 px over 180 ms; exit fades out over 120 ms. With `ReduceMotion` enabled, toasts appear and leave immediately with no fade or movement.
  - Both toast hosts are polite live regions (`core.RoleStatus`, `core.LiveRegionPolite`). Showing a toast never steals focus; `F6` / `Shift-F6` reaches `RegionToast`, `Tab` moves between its action and dismiss buttons, and `Esc` while focused dismisses the toast and restores focus to the control focused before entering the toast.
- **Spec Feedback channels table (`SpecFeedbackChannels`)**:

| Event | Channel | Action / Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Review kept or reverted | Toast | `Undo ⌘Z` |
| Fix applied from Problems | Toast | `Undo ⌘Z` |
| Agent stopped with `⌘.` | Toast | `Review` |
| “Always” permission rule saved | Toast | `Show` |
| Copied a config, path or snippet | Toast | Info (routed from Connect, copy YAML, copy objects, web URL, PR description, diagnostics) |
| A file changed on disk outside the editor | Toast | Warning · `Show` (routed from disk watcher reload) |
| Save or export failed | Toast | Error · `Retry`, stays (explicit `⌘S` / Save and export failures only; failed autosaves stay in the status bar per `Checkpoints.dc.html` and never toast) |
| Export running | Progress toast | `Cancel`, stays until done then turns into `"Exported <path>"` with `Show in Finder` |
| Agent asks permission or a question | Agent panel card | Never a toast |
