| Agent finished a request | Review opens | Never a toast |
| Check failed, broken reference | Problems | Never a toast |
| Agent asks, finishes or stops while the editor is in the background | + System notification | Paired with its in-editor channel |
## Agent automations (#1029)

Project automations (`automations.yaml` beside `game.yaml`, schema `https://game.nsgo.dev/schemas/automations.v1.json`) let the agent run follow-up checks and tasks after a change while keeping every result reviewable (`design/editor/Settings.dc.html`, `Tasks.dc.html`, `Checkpoints.dc.html`):

- **Versioned YAML format (`AutomationsFile`, `ParseAutomations`, `EncodeAutomations`)**: `version: 1` with an ordered `automations` list of `{id, name, enabled, trigger, instruction}`. Unknown fields, trailing documents, duplicate keys, YAML anchors/aliases, duplicate IDs, and invalid trigger options are refused with `*loaderr.Error` line/column diagnostics; encoding is deterministic and round-trips byte for byte.
- **Five triggers**:
  - `file_changed` (`glob`): fires when a matching project file is saved (manual save, autosave, or tab save), supporting `*`, `**`, and `?`.
  - `review_kept` (optional `glob`): fires when an edit review is kept (`KeepReview`, `EndReview(true)`, `KeepTileReview`, `KeepCutsceneReview`, `KeepAllReviewChanges`, `KeepAgentTurnReview`), skipping the originating automation ID to avoid recursion.
  - `file_added` (`kind`, optional `glob`): fires when a new file of kind `prefab`, `scene`, `tileset`, `cutscene`, `material`, `particles`, `rig`, `screen`, `datatable`, `script`, `asset`, or `any` is created (`NewScene`) or detected by disk watching (`PollDiskChanges`).
  - `before_export` (optional `platform`): fires before `StartExportBuild` or `StartReleaseBuild`.
  - `daily` (`time` in 24-hour `HH:MM`): fires once per calendar day at or after `time` while the editor is open (driven periodically by the UI scope scheduler and deterministically via `Editor.StepAutomations`).
- **Pre-run checkpoints, permissions, background tasks, and Ready to review**: before starting any automation task, the editor snapshots tracked project text/YAML/Go files (excluding You-only and internal state files, bounded by file count and total size, skipping symlinks) into `.nsgo/checkpoints/<id>/` (git-ignored, inspectable via `AutomationCheckpoints`, restorable via `RestoreAutomationCheckpoint` with symlink containment checks). Each run executes under bounded concurrency with a timeout, under `WithAgentActor` and the project's permission rules (`Editor.Permissions` / `CheckAutomationActionPermission`), appears in the shared background task list (`TaskSourceAutomation`) and as an `AgentTurn` in **Ready to review** (`AgentReviewReady`) inside the Agent panel with **Keep**, **Dismiss**, and **Restore checkpoint** controls. `automations.yaml` and `.nsgo/automations-state.yaml` are protected by the You-only trust boundary (`YouOnlyAutomations`), and automations can never apply You-only drafts (`PermissionsDraft`, `AutomationDraft`), confirm held loosenings or proposed deletions, or keep/modify their own reviews (`ErrAutomationYouOnly`).
- **Settings › Automations (`SettingsPageAutomations`)**: lists `WHEN → THE AGENT` cards with weekly run counts (`AutomationWeeklyRuns`, stored locally in git-ignored `.nsgo/automations-state.yaml`) and `SpecSwitch` (`core.RoleSwitch`) toggles, **+ New automation, in words** (`DraftAutomationFromWords` -> reviewable YAML card -> human **Keep** via `KeepAutomationDraft`), **Pause all automations for today** (`PauseAutomationsForToday` / `ResumeAutomations` — stops every trigger until the next calendar day), and the **Agent on this page** card, styled exclusively with `theme.Palette` tokens in Dark and Light appearances.
## Change-by-change agent turn review (#1014)

When an agent completes a turn (`Editor.StartTurnReview`), the full-window **Review** screen (`ReviewScreenView`, `design/editor/Review.dc.html`, styled strictly with `Spec.dc.html` tokens in both Dark and Light appearances) lets the author inspect every change across scenes, prefabs, data tables, and Go files one by one before keeping them in a single undo step:

- **Header and blockers**: displays **← Editor** (`Esc`), the quoted agent request, an optional blocker warning pill (when a conflict, YAML error, or other blocker prevents keeping), **Revert all** (`⌘⌫` / `Ctrl+Backspace`), and **Keep N of M** (`⌘↵` / `Ctrl+Enter`). When any blocker is active, **Keep** refuses to commit and jumps focus straight to the blocking change (`JumpToReviewBlocker`).
- **Grouped left list and filters**: pairs a live search input (`SetReviewSearch`, matching name, field, detail, or file path), a `"N of M seen"` counter with progress bar and Previous/Next buttons, and six counted filter chips (**All**, **Added**, **Changed**, **Removed**, **Unseen**, **Notes** via `SetReviewFilter`). Changes are grouped by **file** (path, `+n ~n −n` counts, fold toggle, **Revert file** / **Restore file**), then by **kind** (**ADDED**, **CHANGED**, **REMOVED** with fold and **Revert n** / **Restore n**), then one card per change with a seen mark, semantic kind mark, title, detail, and **Revert** / **Restore** (`X`). Focusing any change marks it seen.
- **Keyboard scope (`ScopeReview`) and navigation**: registers `J` (`CmdReviewNext`), `K` (`CmdReviewPrev`), `X` (`CmdReviewToggleRevert`, toggling the focused change, kind group, or file group), `B` (`CmdReviewPeekBefore`, hold `B` to peek at the scene as it was before the request), `⌘↵` (`CmdReviewKeep`), and `⌘⌫` (`CmdReviewRevertAll`) in `ScopeReview`. Every control shows the Spec 2 px focus ring and is reachable via `Tab`, `F6` / `Shift-F6` (cycling Header → List → Preview → Why & YAML), or arrows; typing in the filter field never triggers single-letter shortcuts.
- **Adaptive center preview**:
  - **Scene files**: renders the focused change's scene (`BuiltScene`) with clickable top-left overlay legend toggles (`ToggleReviewOverlayLegend`), draws unfocused changes at `1.5 px` and the focused change in its **own** semantic color and dash at `2.5 px` with an outlined pill (never Spec accent, which remains reserved for selection), draws reverted changes as they were before the request, frames each focused object on `J`/`K`, and shows `"Before this request"` while holding `B`.
  - **Prefab files**: shows before → after field values with numeric `Δ%` and computes where the change applies across both scenes in the current review and existing project scenes via `Editor.FindUsages`.
  - **Small data tables**: renders the table (`ReviewDataTablePreviewFor`) with changed rows highlighted and modified cells showing `old → new Δ%`.
- **Right pane (Why + YAML)**: shows the agent's reason (**WHY**) above the line-numbered YAML lines touched by the focused change with `+` / `−` signs.
- **Esc / Resume pending state**: pressing `Esc` (`PauseReview`) returns to the editor while leaving the review pending—the main viewport renders pending change overlays (`drawPendingReviewOverlay`) and the right-rail **Agent** tab (`AgentPanelView`) shows `"Review waiting · N changes, F files"` with seen count, blockers, and **Resume review** (`ResumeReview`), which returns to the exact same focused change and seen state.
- **Keep, Revert all, and one-step Undo**: **Revert all** asks in a focus-trapped confirmation dialog naming every file that goes back and reminding that `⌘Z` / `Ctrl+Z` brings everything back. Both **Keep** (`KeepTurnReview`) and **Revert all** (`ConfirmReviewRevertAll`) execute as **one `Editor.History` step**, update saved YAML files and the open scene, show a Spec toast with **Undo**, and record `"Kept K · reverted R · one undo step"` in the Agent panel; a single `Editor.Undo()` restores every touched file byte for byte.
- **MCP read access (`editormcp`)**: the read-only `review_status` tool (`ToolReviewStatus`, `ActionRead`) lets connected agents inspect which changes have been seen or reverted, overlapping conflict choices (`mine`, `agent`, `both`), hand-edited YAML fields and lines tagged `"you"`, active blockers, and keep/revert outcomes (including after Keep for round 2) without granting the agent permission to keep or revert the turn (unsent draft notes are never exposed, and `SceneAndData` / `GoSource` privacy rules are enforced).

### Review notes and second-round redo (#1031)

Instead of reverting everything or hand-editing YAML when one or two changes need adjustment, authors can leave change-scoped notes on the active turn and have the agent redo only those changes as **Round 2** within the same review (`design/editor/Review.dc.html`):

- **Inline note composer (`C` / `CmdReviewNote`) and viewport pins**: pressing `C` on a focused change (or clicking its speech-bubble button) opens an inline note composer under the card (`"Note for the agent · not sent"`) with **Add note** (`CommitReviewNote`), **Revert and redo with this note** (`RevertAndRedoReviewChange`), and **Cancel** (`CancelReviewNoteComposer`, or `Esc`). Saved notes appear on the card with **Edit** and **Remove** (`RemoveReviewNote`) and as Spec-token speech-bubble pins beside noted objects in both the Review viewport and the paused 2D viewport (`ReviewViewportPins`). Notes live strictly in memory on the turn, are bounded in length and count, and are never written to any scene, prefab, or data file; a note added and removed before sending never reaches the agent.
- **Send notes and noted-only redo (`SendReviewNotes`, `ApplyReviewRedo`, `editormcp`)**: clicking **Send N notes** in the header (which becomes the primary accent button while unsent notes exist) dispatches all remaining notes to the agent over MCP (`review_notes_get`), naming each change's `change_id`, `file`, `object`, `field`, `kind`, and `note`. The agent revises only noted changes within the same turn via `review_redo` (un-noted changes and tampered un-noted objects in the same file are refused, You-only/permission/privacy rules are enforced). Redone changes appear unseen again in Round 2+ with `"Redone from your note: …"` in **WHY** and `"Revised in round N. Your note: …"` on the card.
- **Round tabs, banner, filters, paused card, and single-step Undo**: once Round 2 exists, the header shows **Round 1 | Round 2** tabs (`SetReviewViewRound`) and a banner below the filter chips (`"Round 2 · the agent redid only the N changes you left notes on. Both rounds keep and undo as one step."`). **Round 1** is read-only (`ErrReviewReadOnlyRound` on edit/revert/note attempts) and shows the agent's first version for comparison; **Keep** (`⌘↵`) always applies the **latest round** as **one `Editor.History` step**, so a single **Undo** (`⌘Z`) restores every touched file to its exact pre-request bytes. The **Notes** chip filters to changes with notes, the paused **Agent** panel card shows unsent note counts (`"N notes not sent"`), and the **Revert all** dialog warns that pending notes will be dropped.

### Review conflicts and in-place YAML hand editing (#1032)

Inside the change-by-change Review screen, two cases block **Keep** cleanly without losing either the author's or the agent's work:

- **Three-way conflict resolution (`syncReviewAfterMutate`, `SyncReviewPersonEdits`, `ResolveReviewConflict`)**: manual edits made through editor commands (`MoveObject`, `SetProperty`, `SetComponentField`, and so on) while a review turn is pending automatically three-way merge against the turn baseline and agent snapshot via `sceneformat.Merge`. Edits to **different fields** of the same object merge cleanly without asking; overlapping edits to the **same field or list** (such as appending to `door_1`'s `on_open` list) display both sides inside the change card with line authors (`you`, `agent`) and a **Mine / Agent's / Both** segmented control (`ReviewConflictMine`, `ReviewConflictAgent`, `ReviewConflictBoth`), where **Both** keeps both entries with **yours first**. Until every unresolved conflict is chosen, **Keep** is disabled (`*ReviewBlockedError` wrapping `ErrReviewBlocked`) with `"Choose how <object> merges"`, and pressing **Keep** or the header blocker jumps straight to that change.
- **In-place YAML editing and live viewport preview (`BeginReviewYAMLEdit`, `SetReviewYAMLDraft`, `FinishReviewYAMLEdit`)**: clicking **Edit** on any non-reverted added or changed scene object makes the right-hand YAML pane editable in place without losing text-input focus or firing single-letter Review shortcuts while typing. Drafts are bounded (256 KiB), strictly decoded with `KnownFields(true)`, checked for duplicate keys and `sceneformat.Validate` (and component registry rules), and checked against You-only boundaries. Valid edits update the Review viewport live (`ReviewEffectiveSceneDoc`) and tag hand-edited lines `you`.
- **Parse errors, mechanical one-step fixes, and Undo my typing (`ApplyReviewYAMLFix`, `UndoReviewYAMLTyping`)**: invalid YAML (such as typing `x 444` without a colon) marks the failing scene line, surfaces `"Fix YAML on line N"` in the header blocker and `"Line N isn't valid YAML"` in the alert card, disables **Done** (`ErrReviewYAMLInvalid`) and **Keep**, offers verified one-step fixes when the parser names what is missing (**Add the colon**, **Close the quote**, **Add the closing brace**, **Add the closing bracket**, **Replace tabs with spaces**), and always provides **Undo my typing**.
- **Single undo step and round-2 MCP visibility**: conflict merge choices and valid hand edits land with the turn in **one `Editor.History` step** (`KeepTurnReview`)—one `Undo` reverts the agent turn, merge choice, and hand edit together while preserving any concurrent manual edits made outside the review—and remain readable through `editormcp`'s `review_status` tool (`Editor.ReviewStatus`) tagged `"you"` for round 2.

## System notifications, Dock badge, and Play-mode dot for agent tasks (#1023)

Agent tasks (both background tasks managed via `tasks.go` / #1022 and foreground Agent panel runs) reach the person outside the editor through NSGo's native notification and badge APIs (`core.Scope.ShowNotification`, `core.Scope.RequestNotificationPermission`, `core.Scope.OnNotificationPress`, and `core.Scope.SetBadge`), matching `design/editor/Tasks.dc.html`, `design/editor/AgentStates.dc.html`, and `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` using exclusively Spec design tokens:

- **Only three events notify**: **waiting for permission** (`TaskNeedsYou`), **ready to review** (`TaskReadyToReview`), and **failed** (`TaskFailed`), formatted with `"NSGo Editor · <project>"`, the task title, detail, and primary action (`Allow once`, `Review`, or `Open`) with control-character sanitization and length bounds. Finished steps, queued starts, restored thread entries on load, and agent replies never send system notifications.
- **Permission on first need and 30 s rule**: `cx.RequestNotificationPermission` is called only the first time a notification is actually needed (preserving macOS Dock badges before any notification permission check). When the editor window is in the background (`cx.Env().Lifecycle() != core.LifecycleActive`), all three events notify immediately; in the foreground, an unanswered waiting request notifies after `AgentWaitingNotifyDelay` (`30 s`) unless answered first.
- **Click opens the task in its scene**: `cx.OnNotificationPress` (delegating to `Editor.HandleNotificationPress` / `Editor.OpenAgentTask`) brings the editor window to the front (also calling `Editor.OnFocusWindow` when set), stops Play if running, dismisses obstructing panels (Settings, History, Problem agent panel, Ask-in-place, Assets mode), switches to or safely opens the task's scene tab strictly confined inside the project root (refusing traversal and symlink escapes), opens the Tasks or Agent panel, and focuses the task and any open request card.
- **Dock badge (`cx.SetBadge`)**: `Editor.AgentDockBadgeCount` counts waiting (`TaskNeedsYou`), ready to review (`TaskReadyToReview`), and failed (`TaskFailed`) tasks (`AgentTaskCounts.BadgeCount()`); running and queued tasks never add to the badge, and `0` clears it (including on scope cleanup).
- **Play-mode corner dot and deferred notifications (`PlayTaskDotView`)**: while Play mode is active (`Editor.Playing()`), agent task notifications do not pop over the running game and are held (`Editor.HeldAgentNotifications`) until `Editor.Stop()` flushes any tasks still needing attention. A single 8 pt dot with a 3 pt halo stays stably mounted in the top-right corner of the viewport in the Spec color of the most urgent state (**Failed** `Removed.Fg` > **Waiting** `Warning.Fg` > **Ready to review** `Accent.Fg`), revealing its label pill (such as `"1 task needs you · stop Play to open"`) only on hover or keyboard focus.
## Live agent edits in the viewport and scene tree (#1013)

While an agent edits a scene through `editormcp` or an active agent turn (`Editor.BeginAgentTurn` / `Editor.RunAsAgent`), the editor shows its pending changes live in both the viewport and scene tree (`design/editor/Working.dc.html`, `Main.dc.html`, and `Spec.dc.html`) without ever moving the 2D or 3D viewport camera on the agent's behalf:

- **Live viewport overlays (`ViewportLiveOverlays` / `VisibleViewportLiveOverlays`)**:
  - **Added**: 1.5 px dashed green outline (`theme.Palette.Added`) and `"+ <name>"` mono pill.
  - **Changed**: 1.5 px dashed amber outline (`theme.Palette.Changed`) and `"<field> <old> → <new>"` mono pill (also synced into the Inspector's amber review markers).
  - **Removed**: 25% grey ghost at the object's pre-turn bounds, 1.5 px solid red outline (`theme.Palette.Removed`), and a `"<name> removed"` pill with the object's name struck through.
  - **Agent working**: 1.5 px solid pulsing violet outline (`theme.Palette.Agent`, 1.6 s pulse between 45% and 100% opacity, held solid at 100% under reduced motion) with no resize handles and a pill carrying the violet sparkle glyph and activity text (for example `playing level_01 headless`).
  - **Problem**: 2 px solid red outline and filled alert pill for object-scoped diagnostics.
  - Non-overlapping pill placement automatically stacks colliding pills vertically so adjacent objects stay readable.
- **Selection coexistence and read-only step guard**: when the user selects an object the agent is actively touching (`Editor.BeginAgentStep` / `editormcp.Server.BeginStep`), the selection keeps its resize/transform handles and the agent outline sits 3 px further out (`OutsetPx: 6`). Until the agent's step moves on (`EndAgentStep`), manual edits to that object are refused with `ErrAgentTouchingObject` across the viewport, 2D/3D gizmos, inspector (which shows a violet read-only banner), scene tree (rename and reparent), context menu, and direct `Editor` mutation methods, while untouched objects remain freely editable.
- **Scene tree markers**: added nodes show a `NEW` badge, changed nodes show a `MOD` badge, removed nodes remain visible as struck-through ghost rows with a `DEL` badge, and currently touched nodes display a violet agent-working dot until **Keep** or **Revert**.
- **Header pill**: follows the agent's state (`Editor.SetAgentState` / `Editor.AgentState`): violet working pill (`"Agent is <activity> · manual edits stay available"`), warning waiting pill (`"Waiting for you · <detail>"`), pending-review pill with **Keep** (`KeepAgentTurn`, collapsing the turn into one undo step) and **Revert** (`RevertAgentTurn`, undoing the turn and restoring removed objects), and hidden while idle.
- **Interactive top-left Overlay Legend**: appears automatically whenever live overlays are active (or when `OverlayLegendOpen` is true). Each row (**Selection**, **Agent working**, **Added**, **Changed**, **Removed**, **Problem**) is a `RoleSwitch` toggle (`ToggleOverlayLegendKind` / `SetOverlayLegendKind`) persisted per-user in `EditorSettings.HiddenOverlayKinds` (`hiddenOverlayKinds` in `editor.yaml`, schema `editor-settings.v1.json`) without modifying project files.

## Agent threads per line of work, grouped by scene (#1019)

The right rail (`RightRailView`) switches between **Agent** (`AgentPanelView` / `AgentThreadsView`), **Inspector** (`InspectorView`, the default tab), and **History** (`AgentHistoryView`), using `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `Threads.dc.html` across Dark and Light appearances:

- **One YAML file per thread under `.nsgo/threads/` (`AgentThread`, `AgentThreadFileVersion = 1`)**: each thread is persisted as a strict versioned YAML document at `.nsgo/threads/<id>.yaml` (with `.nsgo/.gitignore` automatically ignoring the directory from git), storing `id`, `title` (named from its first request), `scenePath`, external agent `sessionId` (`SetAgentThreadSessionID`), timestamps, and chronological `requests` with `steps`, `outcome` (`pending`, `kept`, `reverted`), change counts, changed `objects`, and `historyStep` links. Parsed with `ParseAgentThreadYAML` and written canonically with `EncodeAgentThreadYAML` (JSON Schema `https://game.nsgo.dev/schemas/agent-thread.v1.json`).
- **Grouped thread switcher, search, and `⌘N` (`AgentThreadGroups`, `CmdNewAgentThread`)**: the switcher groups threads under `<OPEN_SCENE> · OPEN SCENE`, other scenes (`<SCENE>`), and `PROJECT · NO SCENE`, each showing relative age and state (`now · 3 changes to review`, `1 h ago · kept 5 of 6`, `yesterday · reverted`, or changed files). Opening or switching scenes enters that scene's most recent thread (or a fresh draft) and never carries another scene's thread along; while the **Agent** tab is active, `mod+n` / `⌘N` (`ScopeAgent`) starts a new empty thread instead of `File > New Project...`.
- **Folded earlier requests, History link, and Jump to latest**: earlier reviewed requests fold to one line each (`N EARLIER REQUESTS`) and expand on click to show the full prompt, changed fields, steps, and **Open this step in History** (`OpenHistoryStep`), which opens the unified History timeline highlighted on that step. Expanding an earlier request surfaces a floating **Jump to latest** pill (`JumpToLatestAgentRequest`).
- **Empty-thread view (`AgentAlwaysReadItems`, `AgentBriefSuggestions`)**: an empty thread lists what the agent reads with every request (`<scene> · whole scene`, `brief.yaml`, `AGENTS.md`, `Viewport image`) and up to three starter prompts read safely from the project's `brief.yaml` (`FROM YOUR BRIEF`).
- **Inspector footer attribution (`LastAgentChangeForObject`, `OpenAgentRequest`)**: when a selected object was changed by a non-reverted agent request (via `SendAgentRequestWith`, `RecordAgentStep`, or `editormcp`'s `scene_edit`), the Inspector footer displays `Changed in “<request>” · <age>` and clicking it opens that thread and expands that request.
## Viewport sketch layer for the agent (#1025)

Draw session-only numbered annotations directly over the 2D viewport to point at empty places in a level before asking the agent to build them (`design/editor/Sketch.dc.html`, styled exclusively with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens):

- **Four sketch tools in scene coordinates**: press `D` (`CmdSketchToggle`, or `View > Sketch`) to toggle Sketch mode without adding extra buttons to the compact 2D `editor-toolbar`. Switch between **Pen** (`P`, freehand polyline), **Arrow** (`A`, start → end), **Box** (`B`, rect), and **Text** (`T`, point + label) in `ScopeSketch`, or place a mark at the cursor with `I` (`CmdSketchAddMarkAtCursor`). Every mark gets a sequential numbered badge (`1`, `2`, `3`, …) and an optional label (such as `"3 spikes"`, `"platform, back and forth"`, `"locked until the lever"`). All geometry is converted through `CanvasToWorld` and stored strictly in scene coordinates so marks scale and pan accurately across zoom levels and are **never** written into `SceneDoc`, `*.scene.yaml`, or scene undo `History`.
- **Sketch bar, Scene Tree row, and status bar**: the floating sketch bar displays the four tools, **Undo last mark** (`⌘Z` in `ScopeSketch`), **Hide / Show marks** (`H`), `"N marks not saved"`, **Clear**, and **Done**. Pressing `Esc` finishes sketch mode while keeping drawn marks until sent, cleared, or kept. The Scene Tree displays a compact `SKETCH LAYER` row (`N marks · not saved`), and the status bar shows live scene coordinates and the active sketch tool.
- **Sending a sketch to the agent (`SendSketch` / `Editor.OnSendSketch`)**: the composer attaches a `"Sketch · N marks"` context chip (clicking toggles a `"Sent with this request"` scene-coordinate preview). Sending delivers `SketchAgentRequest` with structured marks (`number`, `kind`, geometry in scene coordinates, `label`) and a rendered PNG of the viewport with numbered marks overlaid (`RenderSketchViewportPNG`).
- **Agent reading card & `scene_edit` confirmation hold**: sending a sketch arms a hold gate (`SketchHoldActive() == true`) that causes `scene_edit` and other mutating scene MCP tools to refuse edits until the person confirms. The agent reads marks via `sketch_read` and posts its restatement (`"I read your sketch as: …"` with numbered items and assumptions) via `sketch_reading` (`PostSketchReading`). Clicking **Go ahead** (`ApproveSketchReading`, `⌘↵`) releases the hold; clicking **Correct a mark** (`CorrectSketchMark`, `⇧M`, or clicking any numbered item) reopens sketch mode with that exact mark selected while keeping the hold active; replying in words (`ReplyToSketchReading`) works too.
- **Review overlay & Keep / Revert**: after approval, agent edits open a review card (`SketchReviewOpen`) with a **Sketch overlay** toggle (`O`, `ToggleSketchReviewOverlay`, also available in `TileReview`) that lays the faint dotted sketch over the result. **Keep** (`KeepSketchReview` / `KeepTileReview`) keeps the edits and clears the sketch layer; **Revert** (`RevertSketchReview` / `RevertAllTileReview`) undoes the edits while keeping the sketch marks intact to send again.
## Reference images in the agent composer and pinning to the brief (#1027)

Attach PNG, JPEG, or WebP reference images to a single agent request or pin them to the project's game brief, matching `design/editor/Sketch.dc.html` (*Paste or drop an image*, *Pin to brief*) using exclusively `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens:

- **Drop, paste, or pick PNG, JPEG, and WebP**: dragging files over the Agent composer shows the Spec-styled **"Drop to attach"** overlay (`SetAgentComposerDragOver`, `DropAgentReferenceFiles`); pasting an image or image files into the composer (`HandleAgentComposerPaste` via `core.Input.OnPaste`, or `PasteAgentReferenceImage`) or clicking the **Attach an image** button (`PickAndAttachAgentReferenceImages` / `CmdAttachReferenceImage`) validates actual PNG, JPEG, or WebP headers and pixels in memory (`DecodeAgentReferenceImage`). Unsupported file types (such as GIF, SVG, BMP, text, or corrupted images) are refused with the exact reason in an accessible error banner (`AgentAttachError`).
- **One-shot chips with hover preview**: each attached image appears as a chip with a thumbnail, filename, **Pin to brief** button, and **×** remove button (`RemoveAgentReferenceImage`). Hovering or focusing a chip displays a floating preview card above the composer with the decoded image, format, dimensions, and scope (`Sent with this request only`). Submitting a request (`SubmitAgentPrompt`) delivers attached images in `AgentRequest.AttachedImages` and `AgentRequest.Images` (and records thumbnails on `AgentTurn.Images`), then clears the one-shot chips so the next request does not carry them.
- **Pin to brief (`PinAgentReferenceImage`)**: clicking **Pin to brief** copies the image to `art/reference/<name>` (disambiguating collisions without overwriting different files) and adds an entry under `art.references` in `brief.yaml` via YAML AST editing (preserving existing keys and comments) as **one atomic, undoable `Editor.History` step**. Both files immediately appear in Review / Changes (`RefreshChanges`), a confirmation card displays the `+` YAML diff with an **Undo** button (`UndoLastBriefPin`), and a single Undo removes both the copied file and the YAML line (or deletes a freshly created `brief.yaml` and empty `art/reference/` folder).
- **Always chip (`brief.yaml`)**: whenever `brief.yaml` exists, the composer shows the **Always** row with a locked `brief.yaml` chip (showing the pinned reference count when non-zero). Hovering or pressing the chip previews every pinned reference image, and every subsequent agent request automatically includes those images via `AgentRequest.BriefImages` and `AgentRequest.Images`.
## Agent turns: multi-file pending changes as one undo step (#1011)

Every edit an agent makes through the editor's MCP server between the start and end of one request (started from the Agent panel or opened via `turn_start` and closed via `turn_end`) accumulates into **one pending agent turn** across every file it touches—scenes, prefabs, data tables, `game.yaml`, and animation clips:

- **Live in memory, zero disk writes until Keep + Save**: edits apply in memory immediately so the viewport, scene tree, inspector, and `scene_read` show them live (`Editor.StagedFileYAML`), while nothing is written to disk until the person keeps the turn and saves. Saving (`Save` / `SaveAll`) refuses while a turn is still pending, and autosave pauses while a turn is open.
- **Derived structural diff with `why`**: rather than logging raw commands, the editor structurally diffs each touched file between its pre-turn state and its current state (per object ID and field/component/property/layer/parent for scenes and prefabs, per record for data tables, per dotted key for `game.yaml`, per header/track/event for clips). Each derived `AgentTurnChange` carries `Kind` (`added`, `changed`, `removed`), `Title`, concise `Detail` (such as `z 10 → 12`), `File`, 1-based YAML `Line`, optional `Why` reason (from MCP edit tools' `why` argument), and `Reverted` flag, alongside exact per-file `+added −removed` line counts.
- **Per-change and per-file Revert / Restore while pending**: while a turn is pending or stopped (`⌘.` / `CmdStopAgentTurn`), the person can revert or restore any individual change (`RevertAgentTurnChange` / `RestoreAgentTurnChange` / `ToggleAgentTurnChange`) or every change in a file (`RevertAgentTurnFile` / `RestoreAgentTurnFile`). Reverted changes leave in-memory state immediately and return cleanly on restore, leaving untouched edits (including concurrent person edits on other objects) intact.
- **Keep and Revert as one Undo step + Spec toast**:
  - **Keep** (`KeepAgentTurn` / `⌘↵` / `CmdKeepAgentTurn`) commits all remaining changes across every touched file as **one** `Editor.History` command tagged `AuthorAgent` / `HistoryAuthorAgent` (`History.Entries()`, `History.UndoAuthor()`, `History.RedoAuthor()`). One `⌘Z` restores every touched file byte-for-byte, and `⇧⌘Z` redoes the entire kept turn. A Spec `ToastSuccess` appears with an **Undo (`⌘Z`)** action.
  - **Revert** (`RevertAgentTurn` / `⌘⌫` / `CmdRevertAgentTurn`) restores every touched file to its exact pre-request state and shows a Spec `ToastInfo` whose **Undo** action (`UndoRevertAgentTurn`) brings the turn back as pending.
  - **Checkpoints `⌘Z` rules**: while the agent is actively working (`AgentTurnWorking`), `⌘Z` undoes only the person's own edits; inside an open review (`AgentTurnReviewOpen`), `⌘Z` undoes the person's edits made inside the review first and then reverts the review; once kept or idle, `⌘Z` undoes the ordinary history stack.
- **Right-panel Agent tab & Summary Card (`RightRailView` / `AgentPanelView`)**: switch between **Agent** (`mod+1` / `CmdAgentTab`) and **Inspector** (`mod+2` / `CmdInspectorTab`) in the right panel header (showing a live pending indicator dot and MCP connection status). Styled exclusively with `Spec.dc.html` tokens matching `Main.dc.html` (localized in English and Chinese), the Agent panel displays the request bubble, progress steps, working **Stop (`⌘.`)** control, completion summary, the small summary card (`"6 changes · 1 file · +38 −0"`, grouped changes with per-change and per-file Revert/Restore, **Keep**, **Review**, **Revert**), Kept/Reverted notes (`"one undo step (⌘Z)"`), and the composer with `"Edits stay unsaved until you keep them"`.
- **Navigation, close, and boundary protection**: opening another scene (`RequestOpenScene`), switching scene tabs (`RequestSelectTab`), switching projects (`RequestOpenProject`), closing the window, or quitting with a pending turn prompts **Keep**, **Revert**, or **Cancel** (`pendingTurnDialog`), matching unsaved-changes behavior. Every staged file enforces project-root symlink containment, refuses `.git/**` and You-only files, enforces a 2 MiB per-file limit, and resets cleanly on `OpenProject` and `OpenDemo`.
- **MCP surface (`editormcp`)**: `turn_start`, `turn_end`, `turn_status` (read-only, reporting reverted changes), and `file_edit` (validating `game.yaml`, data tables, clips, prefabs, and scenes in memory) work alongside `scene_edit` and `object_set_field`'s `why` support. By design there is no MCP tool that keeps or reverts a turn.
## Agent panel failure, recovery, and offline states (#1024)

When an agent request does not simply succeed, `AgentPanelView` renders dedicated `design/editor/AgentStates.dc.html` cards using exclusively `theme.Palette` tokens (both Dark and Light themes) while keeping every manual editing tool (Select, Move, Inspector, `CheckProblems`, and Play) working in every state:

- **Partial (`AgentPartialState`)**: displays `"Done N of M · couldn't place <target>"` with the explanation, **Keep these** (keeps partial edits as undoable changes), **Let me point** (`ToggleAgentPointing`, clicking in the 2D viewport picks world coordinates via `PickAgentPointWorld` and `Esc` cancels cleanly), **Try another way** (`TryAnotherWayAgentTurn`), and a change-count review strip (`N changes · 1 file | +X −Y | Review`).
- **Stuck (`AgentStuckState`)**: when the same MCP tool (such as `scene_edit`) fails three times in a row (`AgentStuckFailureThreshold = 3`), the editor pauses/cancels the active request instead of guessing again, transitions the header chip to `Waiting for you`, lists all three refused calls with their errors in a recessed monospace log, and offers a **Hint for the agent** input with **Continue with hint** (`ContinueAgentWithHint`) or **Stop here** (`StopStuckAgentTurn`, leaving the scene and disk untouched since refused edits never mutate files).
- **Lost (`AgentLostState`)**: when an MCP client disconnects or a model API fails mid-request, completed steps stay intact in an unkept `AgentTurnReview`, the interrupted step is marked `"cut off, nothing written"` with no partly written file on disk, and the card states when and at which step it failed alongside **Reconnect**, **Review N changes**, and **Troubleshoot** (or **Retry now** and fallback switching such as **Use Claude Code** for model errors like 529 overloaded). After reconnecting, the agent asks before picking up at the next step (`ConfirmResumeLostAgentTurn` / `DismissResumeLostAgentTurn`) instead of auto-resuming.
- **Stopped (`AgentStoppedState`)**: stopping a running turn records `DONE` and `NOT DONE` step sections, **Resume** (`ResumeAgentTurn`, which starts strictly at the first not-done step and skips steps already done), and **Review N changes**; completed changes stay unkept until explicitly reviewed and kept.
- **Mismatch (`AgentMismatchReport`)**: the editor automatically checks every turn summary against the actual diff (`CheckAgentSummaryAgainstDiff`) and displays a `WHAT · SUMMARY · REVIEW` table when they disagree, including the auto-derived reason when known (for example, an object added at step 3 and removed at step 5), **Ask why** (`AskAgentWhyMismatch`), and **Open review** (`OpenAgentTurnReview`). **Keep** always applies the actual review diff, never the prose summary.
- **Offline (`AgentStatusOffline`, `SetAgentOffline`)**: displays `"You're offline"` and queues submitted requests with timestamps (`AgentQueuedRequests`, `QueueAgentRequest`, `RemoveQueuedAgentRequest`) while Select, Move, the Inspector, game check, and Play keep working. Returning online never runs queued requests on its own; each queued request waits for an explicit **Send** (`SendQueuedAgentRequest`).
## Spec viewport overlays and toggle legend (#1006)

Every semantic overlay drawn over the 2D and 3D viewports passes through one shared helper (`DrawObjectOverlay`) using exclusively `theme.Palette` tokens (`design/editor/Spec.dc.html`, "Overlay legend"), with a non-color second cue (corner handles, pill glyph/prefix, ghost fill, strikethrough, or filled alert badge) so every kind remains distinguishable in greyscale:

| Kind (`OverlayKind`) | Token & line style | Second cue |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Selection** (`selection`) | `Accent`, 1.5 px solid | Four 8×8 corner handles and an accent name tag (person's selection only) |
| **Agent working** (`agent`) | `Agent`, 1.5 px solid, no handles | Pulsing 1.6 s between 100% and 45%, dark pill with 4-point agent glyph (or numbered task badge) saying what the agent is doing |
| **Added** (`added`) | `Added`, 1.5 px dashed | Dark pill labelled `"+ <name>"` |
| **Changed** (`changed`) | `Changed`, 1.5 px dashed | Dark pill showing field, old, and new value (for example `"z 10 → 12"`) |
| **Removed** (`removed`) | `Removed`, 1.5 px solid | 25% grey ghost fill and dark pill with struck-through object name + `"removed"` |
| **Problem** (`problem`) | `Problem`, 2 px solid on the live object | Filled pill (`Problem.Fg` / `Problem.OnFill`) with alert triangle icon and message; never resembles a removed ghost |
| **Focused change in Review** (`focused`) | Base change kind's own color and dash at 2.5 px (never `Accent`) | Bright outlined pill matching the change kind |

- **Pairing, motion, and reduced motion**: When **Selection** and **Agent working** sit on the same object (`PairedWithSelection`), Selection keeps its four corner handles and the Agent outline sits 3 px further out (`OverlaySelectionAgentGap`) with its pill stacked above. Added, Changed, and Removed overlays fade in over 240 ms (`OverlayChangeFadeDuration` / `OverlayFadeAlpha`), and active Agent working outlines pulse on a 1.6 s cycle (`OverlayAgentPulsePeriod` / `OverlayAgentPulseAlpha`). With reduced motion enabled (`core.Environment.ReduceMotion` or `Editor.SetReduceMotion(true)`), change overlays appear immediately at 100% and Agent outlines stay solid at 100% with no animation timer running.
- **Top-left toggle legend (`viewport-overlay-legend`)**: Every edit-mode viewport (both 2D and 3D) mounts the same top-left legend card (`viewportOverlayLegendView`) with six accessible `role="switch"` rows (**Selection**, **Agent working**, **Added**, **Changed**, **Removed**, **Problem**), each showing its drawn line style and second cue. Toggling any row (`Editor.ToggleOverlayKind`, `Editor.SetOverlayKindVisible`, `Editor.SetOverlayLegendKinds`) hides or shows that kind across all viewports immediately and persists `overlayLegend` in the per-user `EditorSettings` YAML file (`Editor.SettingsPath`, outside the project, never modifying project or scene files).
- **Live scene overlay API and Problems object attribution**: Live agent edits, reviews, and plans attach overlays to objects (or removed ghost bounds) via `Editor.SetSceneOverlays`, `AddSceneOverlay`, `RemoveSceneOverlay`, and `ClearSceneOverlays` (`SceneOverlayEntry`). `assetcheck.Problem` now carries `Object` (also returned by `editormcp`'s `game_check`) for scene object, component, override, and connection diagnostics so Problems naming a live object in the open scene automatically draw the Spec **Problem** overlay on that object in both 2D and 3D viewports.
- **Neutral viewport guides**: The camera frame, camera bounds, camera tether, tile grid, and snap grid use neutral `theme.Palette` tokens (`TextMuted` / `TextFaint` 1 px hairline and `2, 4` dotted lines) that no semantic legend kind uses.
## Agent composer: @-mentions, context chips, and context meter (#1020)

Inside the unified right-rail **Agent** tab (`RightRailView` / `AgentPanelView`, switchable with `⌘1` / `⌘2` via `CmdAgentTab` / `CmdInspectorTab`), `AgentComposerView` (`design/editor/Threads.dc.html`, styled strictly with `Spec.dc.html` tokens) adds `@`-mentions, explicit context chips, exact hover previews, and a live context meter:

- **`@`-mention picker over the project search index**: typing `@` in the composer opens a live picker backed by `search.go` (`ensureProjectIndex`), `Editor.Problems`, `PlaytestSession` / `AddComposerPlaytestRun`, and `Editor.History`, showing a match count and 8 groups (**Objects**, **Files**, **Prefabs**, **Assets**, **Problems**, **Playtest runs**, **History steps**, **Rules**) with 9 tabs (**All** plus each group). Navigate with `↑` / `↓` (`MoveMentionSelection`), add with `↵` (`AcceptActiveMention`), cycle groups with `Tab` / `Shift+Tab` (`CycleMentionPickerGroup`), and close with `Esc` (`CloseMentionPicker`). Already-attached items are filtered out.
- **Context chips and exact hover preview**: above the composer, attached chips show what joins the request (removable by clicking `×` via `RemoveComposerChip` or pressing `⌫` in an empty composer via `RemoveLastComposerChip`), **Always** chips show `brief.yaml` and `AGENTS.md` when present in the project root, and **Viewport image** is a toggleable chip (`SetViewportImageIncluded` / `ToggleViewportImage`). Selecting an object in the viewport or scene tree only surfaces a dashed `+ <Name> · selected` suggestion chip (`SuggestedChip`)—nothing joins the request unless explicitly added (`AcceptSuggestedChip`). `TriggerAskAgentShortcut` attaches the selected object or, when nothing is selected, the whole open scene (`AttachWholeSceneChip`) and focuses the composer. Hovering any chip (`SetHoveredChip` / `HoveredChipPayload`) displays the exact header (`# level_01.scene.yaml · lines 72–78`) and YAML text or image bytes resolved by `ResolveChipPayload`.
- **Single-source request assembly and runner delivery (`RenderAgentModelInput`, #1742)**: `AssembleAgentRequest` builds `AgentRequest` from the active chips and nothing else—calling the exact same `ResolveChipPayload` function used on hover—and feeds both `SendComposerPrompt` (`Editor.OnSendAgentComposerRequest`) and `SubmitAgentPrompt` (`Editor.AgentRunner`). Every production runner renders `AgentRequest` through `RenderAgentModelInput` so the chips previewed on hover reach the model verbatim:
  - **Claude Code (`CLIAgentRunner`)**: sends **Always** chips (`brief.yaml`, `AGENTS.md`) via `--append-system-prompt`, sends the per-turn context block (compacted/seed summary, explicitly attached chips, viewport header) followed by the prompt after `--`, and materializes the viewport PNG and any ephemeral reference images into a private temporary directory listed under `Reference images:` and cleaned up when the run exits.
  - **Codex (`CLIAgentRunner`)**: sends the full combined context block (Always chips, compacted/seed summary, attached chips, viewport header) followed by the prompt after `--`, passing validated project image paths and the materialized viewport PNG via `-i <path>` on fresh `codex exec` runs and listing them under `Reference images:` on `codex exec resume`.
  - **Built-in Anthropic API runner**: puts the `editormcp.Server` instructions (via `AgentToolInstructionsProvider`) and **Always** chips in the `system` prompt, sends the per-turn context block and prompt alongside base64 `image` blocks for reference images, attached image-asset chips, and the viewport PNG (honoring `PrivacyViewportImages`), and keeps a bounded per-thread conversation history (`user` / `assistant` turns with tool calls/results and earlier base64 images stripped) that carries follow-up turns, truncates/restores with Rewind/Redo, and drops cleanly on **Compact thread** or **Start fresh with summary**.
- **Context meter ring, breakdown, Compact, and Start fresh**: a 20×20 circular progress ring reflects reported token usage (`SetAgentTokenUsage` or `AgentResponse.Usage`) across three Spec states (normal `≤ 70%`, amber warning `71–89%`, and critical `≥ 90%` `"compact now"`). Clicking the ring opens a 6-row breakdown (**Conversation**, **Open scene**, **Attached**, **Always**, **Tools and instructions**, **Viewport image**) with **Compact thread** (`CompactAgentThread`, folding earlier requests into a summary while keeping the last request whole and dropping the ring) and **Start fresh with summary** (`StartFreshAgentThread`, opening a new thread seeded with that summary).
## AI-first main layout (#1007)

The editor's main window (`main_layout_ui.go`, `ui.go`, `agent_ui.go`, `overlays_ui.go`, `scenetree.go`) implements the AI-first workspace from `design/editor/Main.dc.html` using exclusively `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens, sizing, and accessible controls:

- **44 px header (`mainHeaderView`)**: displays the `<project> / <scene>` breadcrumb (`ProjectDisplayName`, `SceneBreadcrumbName`) with a Scene file menu and unsaved dot (`aria-label: "unsaved changes"` / `"save failed"`), a centered tool `base.RadioGroup` (`Select V`, `Move W`, `Rotate E`, `Scale R`, `Pan Space` as icon-only Lucide 1.8 px@15 px `currentColor` icons with `"Label · key"` tooltips), a `2D / 3D` segmented radio group, `History (⌘Y)` icon button, `Search ⌘K` palette button, and `Play (⌘P)` as the single primary button.
- **232 px left rail (`leftRailSceneView`)**: combines the `Scene / Assets` switcher with `SCENE · N nodes` (`SceneNodeCount`), a quick filter toggle (`SceneTreeFilter` / `SetSceneTreeFilter` / `SetSceneTreeFilterOpen`) that narrows the scene tree by name, ID, or kind with auto-expanded ancestors, a quick-add `+` menu (`AddSceneRailObject` for empty object, trigger, or light and `AddSceneRailLayer`, all undoable through `History`), scene tree rows carrying kind color dots (`ObjectKindColor`), kind tags (`SceneRailObjectKind`, `LayerKindLabel`), and `NEW` badges on pending-review proposed objects (`IsObjectProposedNew`, `SetProposedNewObjectIDs`) with ellipsis-safe layout so visibility/lock buttons never clip, and a bottom `FILES` section listing the open scene, prefabs/instanced scenes it uses (`SceneUsedPrefabs`), and `game.yaml`. Picking a scene tree row switches the right rail to **Inspector**.
- **Viewport dot grid, non-sprite markers, caption, bottom bar, and overlay legend**: in 2D edit mode, `DrawViewport` draws the neutral Spec 16×16 dot-grid surround (`theme.Palette.ViewportDot`) when no scene background color is set and neutral Spec-token markers for visible non-sprite objects so empty nodes, prefabs without sprites, and triggers stay legible and clickable without colliding with semantic overlay legend hues. `viewportCameraCaptionView` subscribes to `Editor.SubscribeCanvasSize` (`CanvasSizeRevision`) so `Camera · W × H` tracks the camera frame on first layout and every resize and avoids the top-left overlay legend, paired with the bottom bar (`Zoom out`, zoom `%`, `Zoom in`, `Snap` as a `base.Switch` with `RoleSwitch` / `aria-checked`, `Fit`, and `Overlays N`).
- **Command Palette (`⌘K`, `PaletteItems`, `ExecutePaletteItem`)**: searches built-in/custom/plugin commands, plugin tools and panels, project scenes and files, **open-scene nodes** (`node:<id>`, selecting, switching to Inspector, and framing the object), and **Settings pages** (`setting:<page>`), with prefix scopes (`>` commands, `@` nodes, `#` files), empty-query **Recent** history (`PaletteRecent`), and an **Ask the agent** entry (`ask:<query>`) that stages natural-language queries directly in the Agent composer.
- **380 px right panel (`RightRailView`)**: hosts the `Agent` (`⌘1`) and `Inspector` (`⌘2`) radio tabs alongside the thread History toggle and live agent connection chip (`Connected · MCP`, `Working`, `Waiting for you`, `Reconnecting`, `Disconnected`); choosing a tree row switches directly to **Inspector**.
- **32 px status bar (`mainStatusBarView`)**: renders muted text on `bg` with the active layer button (toggling Layers), live 2D pointer coordinates, current tool readout, problem count pill (opening Problems), Output, and Debugger. Secondary views previously hosted on the old toolbar (Settings, Audio Mixer, Input Map, Localization/Strings, Export, Library, Data Table, Project Search, Files, Assets, Animation, Particles, Material, HUD Layout, Terrain, NavMesh) remain reachable through the Command Palette (`⌘K`), Menu bar, Settings, and Status bar.
- **Designed empty, loading, and error states (`WorkspaceState`, `SceneOpenError`)**: every region renders a dedicated state for `WorkspaceNoProject`, `WorkspaceNoScene`, `WorkspaceLoading` (`SetProjectLoading`), and `WorkspaceSceneError` (`SceneOpenError`, `RetryOpenScene`, `ClearSceneOpenError`). Opening a missing or malformed scene YAML renders an alert card with file path, line/column, message, fix guidance, **Retry**, and **Dismiss** instead of a blank viewport.
- **Spec controls and keyboard navigation**: preserves Spec heights (`28 px` rows/tool buttons, `30–32 px` buttons, `44 px` header, `32 px` status bar, no interactive hit area under `28 × 28`), spacing (`4 / 8 / 12 / 16 / 24`), radii (`6 / 8`), `base.RadioGroup` / `base.Radio` and `base.Switch` semantics, 2 px Spec focus rings, accessible names, and `F6` cycling across all five regions (`Header`, `Scene tree`, `Viewport`, `Agent / Inspector`, `Status bar`).
## Agent panel live steps, permission cards, and Stop (#1012)

The **Agent** panel (`AgentPanelView` inside `RightRailView`, `⌘1` / `⌘2`) streams live progress, interactive permission cards, follow-up instructions, and global Stop cancellation using `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens (`Working.dc.html`, `Permissions.dc.html`, `Main.dc.html`, `AgentStates.dc.html`) on top of the project's `permissions.yaml` rules and You-only boundaries:

- **Live steps and `agent_report_step`**: starting a request (`Editor.StartAgentRequest` or `Editor.SubmitAgentPrompt`) immediately renders a gray placeholder step (within 300 ms). As `editormcp.Server` executes tool calls, each step streams in plain words with a violet spinner on the single active step and a green check once done. Agents can also call `agent_report_step` (`text`, optional `objects`, optional `status`) to report steps in their own words, name touched scene object IDs, and receive queued `"Add to this request"` follow-ups.
- **Interactive permission cards over `permissions.yaml`**: when a tool call evaluates to `ask` against `permissions.yaml` (after You-only boundary enforcement), `editormcp.Server` pauses that call on its goroutine without blocking the editor UI and renders an `alertdialog` permission card (`LiveRegionPolite`) in the Agent panel showing the title, why the agent wants it, its effect (such as *"Nothing is saved."*), the rule preview and source, and **Allow once**, **Always**, and **Deny**.
- **Focus and keyboard rules (Spec rules 02, 04, 05)**: a permission card never steals focus unless focus is already inside the Agent panel (`Editor.ShouldFocusAgentPrompt`); clicking the header **Waiting for you** chip focuses it on demand. While focused, `⌘↵` allows once, `⌥↵` persists an **Always** rule to `permissions.yaml` via `Editor.ApproveAlwaysPermission` (with the **Always permission rule saved** toast offering **Show** and **Undo**) so future identical calls run without asking, `⌘⌫` denies, `↑`/`↓` cycle buttons, and `Esc` leaves the card unanswered while the agent keeps waiting.
- **No automatic retry after denial**: denying a permission card records its `(action, target)` key on the active request, renders *"You denied … · continued without it"* with an explicit **Allow now** action, notes the unchecked item in the turn's review summary (such as *"Not playtested"*), and immediately returns a structured non-fatal `denied` response for any repeated identical call in the same request without re-opening a card. Starting a new request clears per-request denials.
- **Add to this request, Stop (`⌘.`), and concurrent manual editing**: while the agent works or waits, manual edits stay available and the bottom status bar reports what the agent is doing (*"manual edits stay available"*). The working footer keeps the **Add to this request** input focus-stable while steps stream (`Editor.AddToAgentRequest`). Pressing **Stop** or `⌘.` from anywhere—modal dialogs included—cancels the running request and any waiting Ask (`Editor.StopAgent`), keeps completed steps and scene edits pending for review (pausing autosave until kept or reverted), and shows the **Agent stopped** toast with **Review**; `Esc` never stops the agent.
## Dockable panels and layout (#919)

`EditorView` builds the workspace on NSGo's `ui.DockArea`, turning the Scene tree, Layers, Asset Browser, Files, Library, Inspector (including the right-rail Agent, Sketch, Tasks, History, Problem Fix, and Tile Review views), Problems, Output, Profiler, Animation, States, Tileset, Terrain, 2D Rig, Particle Emitter, Material Editor, Scene Environment, Data Table, Search, 3D NavMesh, Localization, and every active plugin panel into dockable, resizable panels around the viewport (with `SceneTabsView` above the viewport canvas):

- **Drag to resize, move, and tab**: drag any split or dock handle to resize panels, drag any panel tab into another group to tab them together or onto a group's edge to split beside it, and close panels from their tab close button (the `viewport` panel stays open).
- **Show, hide, and reset from the menu**: open **View > Panels** to check or uncheck any built-in or active plugin panel (`PanelVisible`, `SetPanelVisible`, `TogglePanel`, `Panels`), or choose **View > Reset Layout** / **View > Panels > Reset Layout** (`CmdResetLayout`, `ResetLayout`) to restore the default arrangement.
- **Per-user saved layout (`LayoutFile`, `ParseLayout`, `EncodeLayout`)**: layout changes persist automatically as strict versioned YAML outside any project under `<UserConfigDir>/nsgo-game/layout.yaml` (`DefaultLayoutPath`, configured via `Editor.LayoutPath` / `Editor.SetLayoutPath` and wired automatically by `game editor` and `editorcmd`). Starting the editor again restores saved splits, tab groups, active tabs, sizes, and hidden panels, dropping any panel that no longer exists (for example from a removed plugin).
- **Plugin panel placement**: plugin panels (`Plugin.Panels`) appear at their declared default `Dock` (`DockLeft`, `DockRight`, or `DockBottom`) on first use; once moved or tabbed elsewhere by the user, subsequent launches restore them at their moved position.

## Reviewing the agent's Go changes file by file (#1034)

When an agent turn proposes Go edits alongside scene, prefab, or data YAML changes, the editor opens a dedicated file-by-file code review (`CodeReviewView`, matching `design/editor/ReviewCode.dc.html` and `Review.dc.html` with exclusively `theme.Palette` tokens in both Dark and Light appearances):

- **Pending Go isolation and Go build overlay checks**: Go files proposed during a turn (`ProposeCodeReviewFiles` or `editormcp`'s `code_review_propose`) are held strictly in memory until confirmed and kept—until then, the project on disk and Play keep running the old code. `RunCodeReviewChecks` / `RunCodeReviewChecksAsync` builds a temporary Go build overlay JSON (`BuildGoOverlay`, mapping both cleaned and symlink-resolved project paths so macOS `/var` → `/private/var` works cleanly) and executes `go tool nsgo check <pkg>` and `go tool nsgo test -json <pkg> -count=1` with `GOFLAGS=-overlay=<overlay.json>` against the pending code without touching any project file on disk (injectable via `Editor.CodeReviewCheckRunner`).
- **Split file list and Unified / Split diff**: the left sidebar splits into **PREFABS · IN KEEP** (YAML/prefab files included in Keep) and **GO · CONFIRM EACH FILE** (each Go file with `+n −n` line counts and a **Needs OK** / **OK** badge), above an explanation card (*"Why Go confirms on its own"*). Go diffs toggle between **Unified** (`u`) and **Split** (`s`) layouts with old and new line numbers, right-aligned line tags (`fails test`, `failed here`, `agent edited`, `round 2`), and **Open in `<IDE>`** (`Editor.ExternalEditorName`, `OpenCodeReviewInExternalEditor` via `Editor.OnOpenSourceFile`, `Editor.OnOpenFileAtLine`, or the OS opener).
- **Checks strip, failing test card, and Round 2 fixes in the same turn**: the live status strip (`RoleStatus` + `LiveRegionPolite`) shows `nsgo check`, `nsgo test` pass/fail counts, elapsed duration and package path, **Run again** (`r`), and live streaming progress (`"nsgo test running · M of N"` / `"Agent is fixing · nsgo test running · M of N"` with an `"Agent edited <file>:<line>"` badge). A failing test displays its name, `file:line` failure message, and the agent's explanation, with **Ask the agent to fix** (`AskAgentToFixCodeReview`, `Editor.OnAskCodeReviewFix`, keeping the fix inside the same turn as Round 2 and rerunning checks) and **Open the test** (`OpenCodeReviewTestFile`).
- **Per-file confirmation, Keep, Waiting card, and single-step Undo**: **Confirm `<file>`** (`ConfirmCodeReviewFile`) is disabled and refused with `ErrCodeReviewChecksNotPassed` until both `nsgo check` and `nsgo test` pass for the latest pending code, then toggles to **Confirmed · undo**. **Keep** (`KeepCodeReview` / `⌘↵`) writes kept YAML files and **confirmed** Go files atomically as **one undo step** in `Editor.History` so a single `Editor.Undo()` restores old YAML and Go files and removes newly created Go files. Keeping while Go files remain unconfirmed writes only the YAML changes and leaves a waiting card in the **Agent** panel (`"patrol.go is waiting for your OK"`, explaining what that means in Play, with **Review** to resume directly on the unconfirmed Go file). Pressing `Esc` (`CloseCodeReview`) pauses the review without discarding it, and **Revert all** (`RevertCodeReview` / `⌘⌫`) discards pending changes without touching disk.
- **Command and MCP tools**: `CmdReviewCode` (`agent.reviewCode`) opens or resumes an active Go review. `editormcp` exposes `code_review_propose`, `code_review_status`, and `code_review_run_checks` so agents can propose pending Go files, inspect failures, submit Round 2 fixes in the same turn, and rerun overlay checks off the UI goroutine.
## Rewind an agent thread (#1021)

Rewind an agent conversation to before any earlier sent message (`design/editor/Checkpoints.dc.html`, styled exclusively with `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` tokens):

- **Per-turn file and scene checkpoints**: each composer request (`SubmitAgentPrompt`) snapshots the project's files on disk (skipping `.git`, `.nsgo`, `node_modules`, symlinks, and You-only settings files) alongside the open scene and `game.yaml` before the agent runs, and compacts the diff when the turn finishes (`AgentTurn.ChangedFiles`, `AgentTurn.SessionIDBefore`, `AgentTurn.SessionIDAfter`).
- **Hover or focus "Rewind to here" and three choices**: hovering or focusing any sent user message in `AgentPanelView` reveals **Rewind to here** (`OpenAgentRewindPopover` / `ToggleAgentRewindPopover`), opening the `"Rewind to before this message"` popover (`AgentRewindPreview`) with three choices (`AgentRewindMode`), **Cancel** (or `Esc`), and **Rewind** (`ConfirmAgentRewind` / `RewindAgentTurn`):
  - **Files and conversation (`AgentRewindBoth`)**: restores all files touched by the target and later turns from their checkpoints (synchronizing the open scene, inactive scene tabs, `game.yaml`, and file tree), drops the target and later turns from the thread, and restores `AgentSessionID` to `SessionIDBefore` so the next request continues the session from before the rewound message.
  - **Files only (`AgentRewindFilesOnly`)**: restores the files from the turns' checkpoints while keeping the conversation thread and session unchanged.
  - **Conversation only (`AgentRewindConversationOnly`)**: drops the target and later turns and restores the session ID while leaving all files on disk untouched.
- **Post-rewind banner, Redo, and composer return**: after rewinding, the rewound message returns to the composer highlighted with the Spec accent ring (`AgentComposerRewound`) so you can edit and ask it differently, and a status banner above the composer (`AgentRewindBanner`) summarizes what was rewound with a **Redo** button (`RedoAgentRewind` / `CanRedoAgentRewind`) that restores the files, conversation turns, session ID, and prior composer draft.
## Agent plans with clarifying questions and planned ghosts (#1035)

For larger changes—such as edits touching Go code, creating files, or spanning multiple files under the project's `permissions.yaml` rules (`EvaluatePlanPermissions`, `PlanNeededForEdit`)—an agent proposes a structured plan before modifying any scene or file (`design/editor/Plan.dc.html`, styled strictly with `Spec.dc.html` Dark and Light tokens):

- **MCP plan proposal (`plan_propose` & `plan_step_update`)**: calling `editormcp`'s `plan_propose` tool (`ActionRead`, never mutating files before approval) stages an `AgentPlan` in `PlanPhaseDraft` with a title, dynamic summary (`"5 steps · 3 files · 10 objects · about 2 min · ~35k tokens"`), clarifying questions with visual option previews (`room` sketches or `strip` timelines), numbered steps (files, additions, editable numeric `PlanStepField` steppers with min/max limits, permission badges like `Edit Go` or `Run the game`, planned Scene Tree nodes, and viewport ghosts), and waits until the person approves or stops, returning the structured `AgentPlanDecision` (answers, updated step counts, custom step notes, and removed steps). `plan_step_update` reports per-step execution status and attaches the finished `AgentPlanReview` checklist to the turn's review.
- **Interactive Plan card in the Agent panel (`agentPlanCardView`)**:
  - Clarifying questions render as radio option cards with optional previews; picking an option collapses the question to a single summary row with **Change** (`ReopenPlanQuestion`). **Approve (`⌘↵`)** stays disabled and refused (`ApproveAgentPlan`) until every question is answered; **Just do it** (`JustDoItAgentPlan`) selects each question's suggested option and records `"Picked for you: …"`.
  - Numbered steps support inline editing (`EditPlanStep`, `AdjustPlanStepField`, `SetPlanStepNote`) and removal/restoration (`RemovePlanStep`, `RestorePlanStep`), striking out exclusive files in **FILES IN THIS PLAN**.
  - The footer states `"Nothing changes until you approve."` along with the permission-derived reason and a clickable **Permissions** link opening **Settings › Permissions** (`SettingsPagePermissions`).
- **Planned viewport ghosts and Scene Tree markers**:
  - `drawPlanGhostsOverlay` draws agent-violet dashed outlines with step-numbered pills (`ResolvedPlanGhosts`), updating live as question answers move ghosts (via `PlanGhostOverride`) or numeric steppers change repeat counts (`RepeatField`), with a top-left legend (`"planned · not in the file yet"`) and a **Plan ghosts** toggle in the bottom floating viewport bar (`TogglePlanGhosts`).
  - `SceneTreeView` displays a `"{N} planned"` count, step badges on touched existing layers, dashed planned node rows, and the **FILES IN THIS PLAN** footer (`ResolvedPlanFiles`).
- **Running, manual-edit pause, Stop, and turn review**:
  - While `PlanPhaseRunning`, the top header pill shows `"Agent is working · step k of N · manual edits to planned objects are paused"`, the active step's ghost switches from dashed to solid, and manual edits to planned objects (`IsObjectPausedByPlan`) are paused until completion or stop.
  - Pressing **Stop**, `Esc`, or `⌘.` before step 1 writes anything (`StopAgentPlan`) leaves every file unchanged (`NothingWritten: true`) and shows `"Stopped before step 1. Nothing was written."` with **Edit plan** (`EditStoppedPlan`) and **Run again** (`RunStoppedPlanAgain`).
  - Once completed (`CompleteAgentPlan` / `plan_step_update` with `complete: true`), the turn's review (`AgentTurn.Plan`, `agentTurnPlanReviewView`) lists every plan step and marks which were done or skipped. Small edits started with `⌘L` continue to set `SkipPlan: true`.

## Design tokens (#1002)

Every color, type size, spacing step, corner radius, height, and motion duration in the editor comes from the embedded YAML theme and constants defined in `pkg/editor/theme/theme.yaml` and `pkg/editor/theme/theme.go`, matching `design/editor/Spec.dc.html` and `design/editor/Viewport3D.dc.html`:

- **Embedded YAML theme (`theme.yaml`, `theme.Core`, `theme.Palette`)**: loaded with `core.ParseTheme` and attached on `EditorView`'s root scope via `cx.SetTheme(theme.Core())`. Every NSGo `core.Palette` and `ui.ColorsOf` role (`Background`, `Surface`, `Raised`, `Line`, `Border`, `Text`, `TextMuted`, `Accent`, `AccentText`, `Success`, `Warning`, `Danger`, `Focus`) maps directly onto the Spec palette in both dark and light appearances, while `Editor.Palette(cx)` (`theme.Dark()`, `theme.Light()`, `theme.PaletteFor(dark)`) exposes the additional Spec roles (`TextPrimary` / text strong, `TextSecondary` / text, `TextMuted` / text secondary, `TextFaint` / text muted, `OnTextPrimary`, `Viewport`, `ViewportDot`, `PillBg`, `HoverOverlay`, `ActiveOverlay`, `PrimaryHover`, `AccentHover`, `AccentPressed`, `Viewport3DGround`, `Viewport3DGrid`, `Accent`, `Agent`, `Added`, `Changed`, `Removed`/`Problem`, `Warning` with dedicated `Fill` and `Edge`, and 3D axis tokens `AxisX`, `AxisY`, `AxisZ`).
- **Dark palette tokens (`Spec.dc.html` & `Viewport3D.dc.html`)**:
  - Surfaces & borders: `bg #101216`, `panel #12141A`, `raised #171A21`, `line #1F232A`, `line raised #262B35`, `viewport #15181D`, `viewportDot #2A2F38`.
  - Text ladder: `text strong #FFFFFF`, `text #D9DDE5`, `text secondary #B4BAC6`, `text muted #8B92A0`. The retired `#6F7684`, `#5D6472`, and `#4A505C` are never used for text.
  - Semantic roles: `accent #3B7BFF` (on-fill ink `#101216` at weight 600; `#FFFFFF` on dark accent or agent is never used), `agent #9B7BFF`, `added #4FD38A`, `changed #E9A84A`, `removed · problem #FF6B6B`, `warning #F2C14E` (on fill `#2A2414`, edge `#4A3F1F`).
  - 3D axes (`Viewport3D.dc.html`): `axisX #FF6B6B`, `axisY #4FD38A`, `axisZ #3B7BFF` (with WCAG-passing light equivalents `#C92A2A`, `#0D7A43`, `#1D5CE6`).
- **Measured WCAG 2.2 contrast table (`theme.ContrastTable`, `theme.ContrastRatio`)**: `TestContrastTableDarkAndLight` computes the WCAG 2.2 luminance contrast ratio of every foreground/background, on-fill, warning-card, pill, overlay, and 3D axis pair directly from `theme.yaml` for both dark and light palettes, failing below `4.5:1` for text/on-fill or `3.0:1` for UI/overlay edges. A dedicated regression test verifies that reverting dark `text_muted` to `#6F7684` fails the check.
- **Type, spacing, shape, heights, and motion constants**:
  - Type scale: Inter title `14 / 600` (`theme.FontTitleSize`, `theme.FontTitleWeight`), body `12.5 / 400` (`theme.FontBodySize`, `theme.FontBodyWeight`), section `11 / 600` at `+6%` tracking (`theme.FontSectionSize`, `theme.FontSectionWeight`, `theme.FontSectionTrack`), JetBrains Mono `11.5` (`theme.FontCodeSize`, `theme.FontFamilyMono`).
  - Spacing: `4`, `8`, `12`, `16`, `24` px only (`theme.Space4`–`theme.Space24`).
  - Radii & geometry: `6` controls (`theme.RadiusControl`), `8` buttons (`theme.RadiusButton`), `12` cards and toasts (`theme.RadiusCard`), `18` dialogs (`theme.RadiusDialog`); heights `28` rows/tool buttons (`theme.RowHeight`, minimum hit area `theme.MinHitSize = 28`), `30`–`32` buttons (`theme.ButtonHeight`, `theme.ButtonTallHeight`), `44` header (`theme.HeaderHeight`); `1` px inner borders (`theme.BorderWidth`).
  - Motion: `120 ms` hover/press (`theme.MotionHover`), `180 ms` panels/popovers/toasts (`theme.MotionPanel`), `240 ms` agent overlays (`theme.MotionOverlay`), `1.6 s` agent pulse (`theme.MotionAgentPulse`, `100%` ↔ `45%` opacity). `theme.MotionDuration` and `theme.AgentPulseOpacity` disable all motion (`0` duration, solid `1.0` opacity) whenever `Env.ReduceMotion` is enabled.
- **No hardcoded UI color literals or dark-only package aliases**: every non-test `.go` file in `pkg/editor` outside `theme/theme.go` draws UI chrome, gizmos, overlays, and debug views exclusively from reactive `theme.Palette` tokens (`ed.Palette(cx)` / `e.activePalette()`, keeping scene content data such as `scene.BackgroundColor` as scene data). `TestSpecNoHardcodedColorLiteralsInEditor` rejects any `core.RGB`/`core.RGBA` call or hardcoded UI hex color literal outside `theme/theme.go`, and `TestSpecNoDarkOnlyPackageColorVarsInEditor` rejects package-level color variables initialized from `theme.Dark()` or `theme.Light()` so every editor screen repaints live across Dark and Light appearances.
